### [1] [Field-level multi-tracers simulation-based inference of cosmological parameters from 3D maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26210)
*Giulio Scelfo,Satvik Mishra,Mauro Rigo,Roberto Trotta,Matteo Viel*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper demonstrates a Simulation-Based Inference (SBI) pipeline that uses neural emulators trained on hydrodynamical simulations to infer cosmological parameters $\{Ω_{\rm m}, σ_8\}$ from field-level galaxy and neutral hydrogen (HI) intensity mapping data. By moving beyond traditional summary statistics (e.g., power spectrum) to full 2D/3D maps and combining multiple tracers, the authors achieve up to a 7-fold improvement in cosmological Figure of Merit, with a consistent ~3× gain from field-level inference alone, even after marginalizing over astrophysical uncertainties.
\n\n
本文展示了一种基于模拟的推理（Simulation-Based Inference, SBI）流程，该流程利用在流体动力学模拟上训练的神经网络模拟器（neural emulators），从星系统计和中性氢（HI）强度映射的场级（field-level）数据中推断宇宙学参数 $\{Ω_{\rm m}, σ_8\}$。通过超越传统的汇总统计量（如功率谱），转而使用完整的2D/3D场，并结合多示踪剂信息，作者在宇宙学参数联合精度的Figure of Merit上实现了最高达7倍的提升；仅靠场级推理即可获得约3倍的一致性增益，即使在对天体物理参数进行边缘化后依然稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current likelihood-based cosmological inference relies heavily on low-order summary statistics (e.g., power spectrum), which discard significant field-level information present in large-scale structure (LSS) data. Upcoming surveys will deliver high-fidelity 3D maps of galaxies and HI, making it imperative to develop methods that can exploit the full statistical content of these fields while accounting for complex baryonic physics modeled in hydrodynamical simulations. Simulation-Based Inference offers a promising framework to achieve this by bypassing explicit likelihoods and directly learning the posterior from simulations.
\n\n
当前基于似然的宇宙学推断严重依赖低阶汇总统计量（例如功率谱），这会丢弃大尺度结构（LSS）数据中存在的大量场级信息。未来的巡天将提供高保真的星系和HI三维地图，因此迫切需要开发能够利用这些场的完整统计信息、同时考虑流体动力学模拟中所包含的复杂重子物理效应的方法。Simulation-Based Inference（SBI）提供了一个有前景的框架，通过绕过显式似然函数，直接从模拟中学习后验分布，从而实现这一目标。

Method: The authors construct a proof-of-concept SBI pipeline comprising: (1) fast approximate dark matter-only simulations; (2) neural emulators trained on high-resolution hydrodynamical simulations to map dark matter fields to realistic galaxy number density and HI intensity fields; (3) neural posterior estimation (NPE), a likelihood-free inference technique, to infer cosmological parameters $\{Ω_{\rm m}, σ_8\}$ from simulated observables. They compare inference based on the power spectrum versus compressed representations of full 2D/3D maps, and analyze single-tracer (galaxy or HI) versus multi-tracer (combined) scenarios, while marginalizing over astrophysical nuisance parameters encoded in the emulator.
\n\n
作者构建了一个概念验证型SBI流程，包括：(1) 快速近似的纯暗物质模拟；(2) 在高分辨率流体动力学模拟上训练的神经网络模拟器，用于将暗物质场映射为真实的星系数密度场和HI强度场；(3) 使用神经后验估计（Neural Posterior Estimation, NPE）——一种无似然推断技术——从模拟观测数据中推断宇宙学参数 $\{Ω_{\rm m}, σ_8\}$。他们比较了基于功率谱与基于完整2D/3D场压缩表示的推断效果，并分析了单示踪剂（星系或HI）与多示踪剂（联合）情形，同时对编码在模拟器中的天体物理干扰参数进行边缘化。

Result: Combining galaxy and HI fields improves cosmological constraints by a factor of 2–7 in terms of the Figure of Merit (FoM) compared to single-tracer analyses, depending on the data configuration. Field-level inference using 3D maps yields consistently tighter and better-calibrated posteriors than power spectrum–based inference, providing an additional ~3× gain in constraining power. This improvement persists even when marginalizing over astrophysical parameters, demonstrating robustness against modeling uncertainties in baryonic processes.
\n\n
结合星系和HI场相较于单示踪剂分析，在宇宙学参数联合精度的Figure of Merit（FoM）上提升了2至7倍，具体取决于数据构型。使用3D场进行场级推断所得后验分布始终比基于功率谱的推断更紧致且校准更佳，额外带来约3倍的约束力提升。即使在对天体物理参数进行边缘化后，这一改进依然存在，表明该方法对重子物理过程建模不确定性具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Field-level Simulation-Based Inference leveraging multi-tracer information and neural emulators trained on hydrodynamical simulations significantly enhances cosmological parameter constraints beyond traditional summary-statistic approaches. The demonstrated gains highlight the potential of SBI to unlock the full information content of upcoming LSS surveys. Future work must incorporate realistic survey systematics and improve emulator fidelity to enable application to real observational data.
\n\n
利用多示踪剂信息并结合在流体动力学模拟上训练的神经网络模拟器的场级Simulation-Based Inference（SBI），显著超越了传统汇总统计方法在宇宙学参数约束上的能力。所展示的增益凸显了SBI在释放未来大尺度结构（LSS）巡天全部信息潜力方面的前景。未来工作需纳入真实的巡天系统误差并提升模拟器的保真度，以实现对真实观测数据的应用。

Abstract: Extracting maximum cosmological information from current and upcoming large-scale structure data requires going beyond summary statistics as currently used in likelihood-based inference. Simulation-Based Inference (SBI) promises to enable the exploitation of field-level information and the rich physics of modern hydrodynamical simulations. We develop a proof-of-concept SBI pipeline to explore its potential to constrain the cosmological parameters $\{Ω_{\rm m}, σ_8\}$ from galaxy number counts, neutral hydrogen (HI) intensity mapping and their combination. We use neural emulators trained on full hydrodynamical simulations to generate galaxy and HI maps from fast, approximate dark matter simulations. Combined with neural posterior estimation, this enables the estimation of cosmological parameters while marginalizing over astrophysical effects. We perform inference both on the power spectrum and on representations derived from field-level 2D or 3D maps, comparing results from each probe and the combination of both tracers, and assessing the impact of data compression and multi-tracers information on cosmological constraints. Combining galaxy and HI fields improves constraints with respect to single-tracer cases by a factor 2 to 7 in terms of a Figure of Merit describing the joint precision on cosmological parameters, depending on the tracer/configuration. Moving from summary statistics to field-level inference leads to a consistent gain in constraining power of about a factor 3, with 3D maps providing the most precise and well-calibrated posteriors. This gain in precision is robust even when astrophysical parameters are marginalized over. Further developments (including realistic survey effects and improvements in emulators' faithfulness) will enable the application of this analysis pipeline to upcoming surveys.

</details>


### [2] [NuSTAR as an Axion Helioscope: probing axion-nucleon and axion-electron couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26265)
*Tiziano Zanzarella,Francisco R. Candón,Maurizio Giannotti,Marco Regis,Jaime Ruz,Marco Taoso,Elisa Todarello,Julia K. Vogel*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper uses solar X-ray data from NuSTAR during the 2020 solar minimum to set new, stringent constraints on axion and axion-like particle (ALP) couplings. By analyzing photon regeneration from solar axions produced via thermal processes and axion-electron/nucleon interactions, the authors derive upper limits on the coupling products $g_{ae}\cdot g_{aγ}$ and $g_{aN}^{\rm eff}\cdot g_{aγ}$ for ultralight axion masses ($m_a \lesssim 10^{-6}\,\rm eV$), significantly improving upon existing experimental bounds.
\n\n
本文利用2020年太阳极小期期间NuSTAR的太阳X射线数据，对axion（轴子）和axion-like particles (ALP) 的耦合设定了新的严格限制。通过分析由热过程及axion-电子/核子相互作用产生的太阳axion再生成光子的信号，作者对超轻axion质量（$m_a \lesssim 10^{-6}\,\rm eV$）情形下的耦合乘积 $g_{ae}\cdot g_{aγ}$ 和 $g_{aN}^{\rm eff}\cdot g_{aγ}$ 给出了上限，显著优于现有实验限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Axions and axion-like particles (ALPs) are well-motivated beyond-Standard-Model candidates that could solve the strong CP problem and constitute dark matter. Solar observations offer a natural laboratory for probing ALPs because they can be copiously produced in the Sun’s core through Primakoff conversion and couplings to electrons or nucleons, and subsequently reconvert into detectable X-ray photons in the solar magnetic field. Existing terrestrial experiments have limited sensitivity in certain coupling regimes, motivating the use of astrophysical probes like solar X-ray telescopes.
\n\n
Axion和axion-like particles (ALP) 是解决强CP问题并可能构成暗物质的超出标准模型的重要候选者。太阳观测为探测ALP提供了天然实验室，因为ALP可通过Primakoff转换以及与电子或核子的耦合在太阳核心大量产生，并在太阳磁场中重新转换为可探测的X射线光子。现有地面实验在某些耦合区域灵敏度有限，因此有必要利用太阳X射线望远镜等天体物理探针。

Method: The authors analyze archival X-ray data from the Nuclear Spectroscopic Telescope Array (NuSTAR) collected during the 2020 solar minimum—a period of low solar activity that minimizes background contamination. They model solar ALP production via three channels: Primakoff conversion (photon–axion oscillations in EM fields), axion-electron bremsstrahlung, and axion-nucleon interactions. The regenerated X-ray flux from ALP-to-photon reconversion in the solar corona is computed using realistic models of the solar magnetic field. A likelihood analysis is then performed to compare observed X-ray counts with predicted ALP-induced signals, yielding exclusion limits on coupling products $g_{ae}\cdot g_{aγ}$ and $g_{aN}^{\rm eff}\cdot g_{aγ}$ for $m_a \lesssim 10^{-6}\,\rm eV$.
\n\n
作者分析了2020年太阳极小期（太阳活动低、背景污染最小）期间Nuclear Spectroscopic Telescope Array (NuSTAR) 获取的存档X射线数据。他们通过三种机制建模太阳ALP产生：Primakoff转换（电磁场中的光子–axion振荡）、axion-电子轫致辐射和axion-核子相互作用。利用太阳磁场的真实模型，计算了ALP在日冕中再转换为X射线光子所产生的再生通量。随后通过似然分析将观测到的X射线计数与ALP诱导信号的理论预测进行比较，从而对 $m_a \lesssim 10^{-6}\,\rm eV$ 情形下的耦合乘积 $g_{ae}\cdot g_{aγ}$ 和 $g_{aN}^{\rm eff}\cdot g_{aγ}$ 给出排除上限。

Result: The analysis yields 95% confidence level upper limits of $g_{ae}\cdot g_{aγ}\lesssim 1.1\times10^{-24}\,\rm GeV^{-1}$ and $g_{aN}^{\rm eff}\cdot g_{aγ}\lesssim 2.3\times 10^{-19}\,\rm GeV^{-1}$ for axion masses below $10^{-6}\,\rm eV$. These bounds improve upon current ground-based experimental constraints by several orders of magnitude in the respective coupling regimes, demonstrating that solar X-ray observations with NuSTAR provide a highly competitive probe of ALP parameter space.
\n\n
分析得出95%置信度的上限：对于质量低于 $10^{-6}\,\rm eV$ 的axion，$g_{ae}\cdot g_{aγ}\lesssim 1.1\times10^{-24}\,\rm GeV^{-1}$，$g_{aN}^{\rm eff}\cdot g_{aγ}\lesssim 2.3\times 10^{-19}\,\rm GeV^{-1}$。这些限制在相应耦合区域比当前地面实验约束提高了数个数量级，表明利用NuSTAR进行的太阳X射线观测是探索ALP参数空间极具竞争力的方法。

Conclusion: Solar X-ray observations, particularly with high-sensitivity instruments like NuSTAR during periods of low solar activity, constitute a powerful and robust method for constraining axion and ALP couplings. The derived limits significantly surpass existing laboratory bounds for electron- and nucleon-coupled ALP production, highlighting the importance of astrophysical probes in the search for weakly interacting sub-eV particles.
\n\n
太阳X射线观测——尤其是在太阳活动低谷期使用NuSTAR等高灵敏度仪器——是一种强大且稳健的约束axion和ALP耦合的方法。所获得的限制在电子和核子耦合主导的ALP产生机制下显著优于现有实验室界限，凸显了天体物理探针在搜寻弱相互作用亚eV粒子中的关键作用。

Abstract: We investigate solar X-ray observations as a probe of axions and axion-like particles. These particles can be produced in the interior of the Sun via the conversion of thermal photons, as well as through processes involving axion-electron and axion-nucleon interactions. The resulting axions can then reconvert into photons in the Sun's atmospheric magnetic field, generating a signal in the X-ray energy range. In this work, we derive new limits on axions using X-ray observations with the Nuclear Spectroscopic Telescope Array (NuSTAR) during the 2020 solar minimum. In the regime where ALP production is dominated by couplings to electrons or nucleons, we obtain bounds on the product of couplings $g_{ae}\cdot g_{aγ}\lesssim 1.1\times10^{-24}\,\rm GeV^{-1}$ and $g_{aN}^{\rm eff}\cdot g_{aγ}\lesssim 2.3\times 10^{-19}\,\rm GeV^{-1}$ at 95% CL, for axion masses $m_a\lesssim10^{-6}\,\rm eV$. These constraints strongly improve current ground-based experimental limits, establishing solar X-ray observations as a powerful and robust method for axion searches.

</details>


### [3] [Unveiling the dark matter nature with reionization relics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26518)
*Yao Zhang,Paulo Montero-Camacho,Catalina Morales-Gutiérrez,Heyang Long,Christopher M. Hirata,Yi Mao*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper proposes a novel large-scale cosmological probe of Warm Dark Matter (WDM) using "reionization relics"—long-lived fluctuations in Ly$\\alpha$ forest opacity and post-reionization HI 21 cm emission imprinted by patchy reionization. These relics encode WDM effects both through suppressed small-scale structure and altered reionization history. The authors forecast that combining DESI-like Ly$\\alpha$ forest data with SKA 21 cm intensity mapping can constrain the WDM particle mass to $m_{\\rm WDM} > 7.1\\,{\\rm keV}$ (95% CL), significantly improving upon current limits, with next-generation surveys potentially reaching $m_{\\rm WDM} > 39\\,{\\rm keV}$.
\n\n
本文提出了一种利用“再电离遗迹”（由斑块状再电离所印刻的Ly$\\alpha$森林光学深度和再电离后HI 21厘米辐射中的长寿命涨落）来探测温暗物质（Warm Dark Matter, WDM）的新颖大尺度宇宙学探针。这些遗迹通过小尺度结构抑制和再电离历史的改变共同编码了WDM效应。作者预测，结合类似DESI的Ly$\\alpha$森林数据与SKA的21厘米强度映射观测，可将WDM粒子质量限制提升至$m_{\\rm WDM} > 7.1\\,{\\rm keV}$（95%置信度），显著优于当前限制；下一代巡天甚至可能达到$m_{\\rm WDM} > 39\\,{\\rm keV}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current constraints on Warm Dark Matter (WDM) rely heavily on small-scale astrophysical probes like the Ly$\\alpha$ forest and Milky Way satellite counts, which are subject to complex baryonic physics and observational systematics. The authors aim to develop a complementary, large-scale probe less sensitive to these uncertainties by exploiting the coupling between WDM-induced suppression of small-scale power and its impact on the inhomogeneous reionization history of the Universe, which leaves persistent imprints ("reionization relics") on large-scale observables.
\n\n
目前对温暗物质（WDM）的限制主要依赖于Ly$\\alpha$森林和银河系卫星星系计数等小尺度天体物理探针，而这些探针易受复杂重子物理过程和观测系统误差的影响。作者旨在开发一种互补的大尺度探针，通过利用WDM引起的小尺度功率抑制与其对宇宙非均匀再电离历史的影响之间的耦合，从而在大尺度可观测量上留下持久印记（“再电离遗迹”），以降低对上述不确定性的敏感度。

Method: The method involves modeling how WDM modifies both the small-scale matter power spectrum and the timeline/spatial morphology of cosmic reionization. This leads to "reionization relics": residual large-scale fluctuations in Ly$\\alpha$ forest effective optical depth and post-reionization HI 21 cm brightness temperature, even after reionization ends. The authors compute the resulting Ly$\\alpha$ and 21 cm power spectra including these relic contributions for different WDM masses (e.g., 3 keV vs CDM). They then perform a Fisher matrix forecast using a DESI-mimicking Ly$\\alpha$ forest covariance and incorporate future SKA 21 cm intensity mapping to project constraints on $m_{\\rm WDM}$.
\n\n
该方法包括建模WDM如何同时改变小尺度物质功率谱以及宇宙再电离的时间线和空间形态。这导致了“再电离遗迹”：即使在再电离结束后，Ly$\\alpha$森林有效光学深度和再电离后HI 21厘米亮温度中仍存在残余的大尺度涨落。作者计算了包含这些遗迹贡献的Ly$\\alpha$和21厘米功率谱，并针对不同WDM质量（如3 keV vs CDM）进行对比。随后，他们使用模拟DESI观测能力的Ly$\\alpha$森林协方差矩阵进行Fisher矩阵预测，并结合未来SKA的21厘米强度映射观测，投影对$m_{\\rm WDM}$的限制能力。

Result: Including reionization relics, the Ly$\\alpha$ and 21 cm power spectra in a 3 keV WDM model deviate from Cold Dark Matter (CDM) by approximately 19% at $k = 0.05\\,{\\rm Mpc^{-1}}$ at redshifts $z=4$ and $z=5.5$, respectively. Forecasts show that DESI-like Ly$\\alpha$ forest data alone can constrain $m_{\\rm WDM} > 5.0\\,{\\rm keV}$ (95% CL). Combining this with SKA 21 cm intensity mapping improves the limit to $m_{\\rm WDM} > 7.1\\,{\\rm keV}$. Next-generation surveys could push the lower bound up to 39 keV, far exceeding the current best limit of 9.7 keV.
\n\n
包含再电离遗迹后，在3 keV WDM模型中，Ly$\\alpha$和21厘米功率谱在$k = 0.05\\,{\\rm Mpc^{-1}}$处分别于红移$z=4$和$z=5.5$时偏离冷暗物质（CDM）约19%。预测表明，仅使用类似DESI的Ly$\\alpha$森林数据即可将WDM质量限制为$m_{\\rm WDM} > 5.0\\,{\\rm keV}$（95%置信度）；若结合SKA的21厘米强度映射观测，该限制可提升至$m_{\\rm WDM} > 7.1\\,{\\rm keV}$。下一代巡天甚至可将下限推高至39 keV，远超当前最佳限制9.7 keV。

Conclusion: Reionization relics provide a powerful new large-scale probe of Warm Dark Matter that leverages the interplay between small-scale suppression and reionization history. By combining Ly$\\alpha$ forest and 21 cm intensity mapping—two upcoming high-precision cosmological surveys—constraints on WDM particle mass can be dramatically improved, potentially ruling out thermal WDM candidates well below the scale probed by traditional small-scale methods. This opens a new window for testing dark matter physics with large-scale structure.
\n\n
再电离遗迹为探测温暗物质提供了一种强有力的新大尺度探针，它巧妙地结合了小尺度功率抑制与再电离历史之间的相互作用。通过联合Ly$\\alpha$森林与21厘米强度映射——两项即将开展的高精度宇宙学巡天——对WDM粒子质量的限制可显著提升，甚至可能排除传统小尺度方法难以触及的热产生WDM候选者。这为利用大尺度结构检验暗物质物理开辟了新窗口。

Abstract: Dark matter constitutes roughly one-fourth of the Universe, yet its physical nature remains unknown. Warm dark matter (WDM), a class of dark matter candidates, has non-negligible velocity dispersion that suppresses the formation of small-scale cosmic structures. Current constraints therefore rely mainly on small-scale probes such as the Lyman-alpha (Ly$α$) forest and Milky Way observations of satellite galaxies and stellar streams. We propose a novel large-scale probe based on long-lived "reionization relics": because the thermal and dynamical evolution of the intergalactic medium depends on the local reionization redshift, patchy reionization imprints additional large-scale fluctuations in Ly$α$ forest opacity and post-reionization HI traced by 21 cm intensity mapping. The strength of these imprints depends on WDM through both small-scale gas evolution and WDM-driven changes in the reionization history. For example, the Ly$α$ (21 cm) power spectrum in 3 keV WDM differs from cold dark matter by ~19% (~19%) at $k=0.05\,{\rm Mpc^{-1}}$ at z=4 (z=5.5) when reionization relics are included. Using Ly$α$ forest with a covariance model designed to mimic the capabilities of the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI), we forecast a constraint of $m_{\rm WDM}>5.0\,{\rm keV}$ (95%), which improves to $m_{\rm WDM}>7.1\,{\rm keV}$ when combined with 21 cm intensity-mapping observations from the Square Kilometre Array (SKA). The next-generation surveys can further strengthen the current best lower bounds from 9.7 to 39 keV.

</details>


### [4] [Constraining Early Dark Energy cosmological models with Big Bang Nucleosynthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26749)
*Teodora M. Matei,Cristian Croitoru,Tiberiu Harko*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper investigates Early Dark Energy (EDE) models and their compatibility with Big Bang Nucleosynthesis (BBN), using a nested sampling-based simulator (eden) to constrain the maximum allowed dark energy density during the radiation-dominated era. It finds stringent upper limits on EDE density for linear ($<10^{-13}$ MeV$^4$) and polytropic ($<10^{-5}$ MeV$^4$) equations of state, and identifies a temperature-dependent equation of state as the most viable, consistent with BBN for coupling parameters $\lesssim 10^{-2}$.
\n\n
本文研究了早期暗能量（Early Dark Energy, EDE）模型与大爆炸核合成（Big Bang Nucleosynthesis, BBN）的相容性，利用基于嵌套采样算法的模拟器（eden）约束辐射主导时期允许的最大暗能量密度。研究发现，对于线性状态方程和多方状态方程，EDE 密度上限分别为 $<10^{-13}$ MeV$^4$ 和 $<10^{-5}$ MeV$^4$（95% 置信水平），并指出温度依赖的状态方程是最具物理合理性的框架，在耦合参数 $\lesssim 10^{-2}$ 时与原初元素丰度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The Hubble tension between early- and late-universe measurements challenges the completeness of the standard $Λ$CDM model. Early Dark Energy (EDE) has been proposed as a potential resolution by introducing an additional acceleration phase before recombination. However, such modifications must not spoil the successful predictions of Big Bang Nucleosynthesis (BBN). This work aims to systematically assess how different EDE models—characterized by distinct equations of state—affect BBN yields and to derive robust constraints on their energy density during the radiation-dominated epoch.
\n\n
早期宇宙与晚期宇宙对哈勃常数的测量之间存在显著张力（Hubble tension），挑战了标准 $Λ$CDM 模型的完备性。早期暗能量（Early Dark Energy, EDE）被提出作为潜在解决方案，通过在复合之前引入额外的加速阶段来缓解该张力。然而，这类修改不能破坏大爆炸核合成（Big Bang Nucleosynthesis, BBN）的成功预言。本文旨在系统评估不同 EDE 模型（由不同状态方程刻画）对 BBN 元素产率的影响，并在辐射主导时期对 EDE 能量密度施加严格限制。

Method: The authors construct a numerical simulator called eden that integrates modified Friedmann equations incorporating Early Dark Energy with varying equations of state into a BBN code. They employ a nested sampling algorithm to explore the parameter space of EDE models—including constant $w$, linear $w(a)$, polytropic forms, and a novel temperature-dependent $w(T)$—and compute posterior distributions constrained by observed primordial abundances (e.g., D/H, $^4$He). The analysis yields 95% confidence upper bounds on the dark energy density $\\rho_{\\rm DE}$ at BBN temperatures ($T \\sim \\text{MeV}$).
\n\n
作者构建了一个名为 eden 的数值模拟器，将包含不同状态方程的早期暗能量（EDE）修正后的弗里德曼方程整合进大爆炸核合成（BBN）计算程序中。他们采用嵌套采样（nested sampling）算法探索 EDE 模型的参数空间——包括常数状态方程参数 $w$、线性 $w(a)$、多方形式以及一种新颖的温度依赖状态方程 $w(T)$——并利用观测到的原初丰度（如 D/H、$^4$He）约束后验分布。分析得出了在 BBN 温度（$T \\sim \\text{MeV}$）下暗能量密度 $\\rho_{\\rm DE}$ 的 95% 置信度上限。

Result: For a linear equation of state, the dark energy density is constrained to $\\rho_{\\rm DE} < 10^{-13}~\\text{MeV}^4$; for a polytropic equation of state, $\\rho_{\\rm DE} < 10^{-5}~\\text{MeV}^4$, both at 95% confidence. A temperature-dependent equation of state $w(T)$ is found to be the most physically viable: it permits significant EDE contributions at high temperatures (potentially addressing the Hubble tension) while rapidly diluting as the universe cools, thereby preserving standard BBN predictions for coupling strengths $\\lesssim 10^{-2}$.
\n\n
对于线性状态方程，暗能量密度被限制为 $\\rho_{\\rm DE} < 10^{-13}~\\text{MeV}^4$；对于多方状态方程，则为 $\\rho_{\\rm DE} < 10^{-5}~\\text{MeV}^4$，两者均为 95% 置信水平。研究发现温度依赖的状态方程 $w(T)$ 是最具物理可行性的：它允许在高温下存在显著的 EDE 贡献（可能有助于缓解哈勃张力），同时在宇宙冷却时迅速稀释，从而在耦合强度 $\\lesssim 10^{-2}$ 时保持标准 BBN 预言。

Conclusion: Not all Early Dark Energy models are compatible with Big Bang Nucleosynthesis. Stringent constraints rule out many simple parametrizations unless the dark energy density during BBN is extremely suppressed. However, a temperature-dependent equation of state provides a compelling framework that reconciles EDE-driven early acceleration with BBN success, offering a viable path to resolving the Hubble tension without violating primordial nucleosynthesis bounds.
\n\n
并非所有早期暗能量（EDE）模型都与大爆炸核合成（BBN）相容。严格的约束排除了许多简单参数化形式，除非 BBN 时期的暗能量密度被极度压低。然而，温度依赖的状态方程提供了一个有说服力的框架，既能实现 EDE 驱动的早期加速，又能与 BBN 成功预言相容，为在不违反原初核合成限制的前提下解决哈勃张力提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The recent cosmological picture contains a significant tension indicating that our standard $Λ$CDM picture may be incomplete. Early Dark Energy models can alleviate the Hubble tension, by assuming an early acceleration that could explain the divergence between the early and late-time cosmological data. We investigate the implications of Early Dark Energy models on the Big Bang Nucleosynthesis processes by considering several cosmological models, including a model assuming a simple cosmological constant, alongside with varying equations of state dark energy models. We construct a simulator through a nested sampling algorithm, with the help of which we estimate the upper bounds for model parameters, and determine the maximum allowable dark energy density contribution during the radiation-dominated era. Our results are obtained through the \href{https://github.com/croi900/eden}{eden} program. We show that for a linear or polytropic equation of state, the dark energy density is constrained to less than $10^{-13}$ MeV$^4$ and $10^{-5}$ MeV$^4$, respectively, at the 95\% confidence level. Furthermore, we identify a temperature-dependent equation of state of dark energy as the most physically compelling framework, which remains consistent with primordial abundances for coupling parameters $\lesssim 10^{-2}$. This model successfully allows for high-temperature deviations from the standard $Λ$CDM expansion history, while rapidly diluting to obtain standard general relativistic results in the weak freeze-out era.

*Zhujun Jiang,Xu Xiao,Fenfen Yin,Xiao-Dong Li,Le Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: We propose $R^{\rm wst}$, a bias-robust statistic derived from wavelet scattering transform (WST) $m$-mode ratios, which enables precision cosmological parameter inference without explicit tracer-bias modeling. Validated on \texttt{Kun} and \texttt{JiuTian} simulations, it achieves percent-level emulator accuracy, yields unbiased constraints on $Ω_m$, $σ_8$, $n_s$, and $w_0$, and improves the $Ω_m$--$σ_8$ degeneracy breaking by a factor of two over the 2PCF, while remaining stable across diverse bias scenarios.

我们提出了一种基于小波散射变换（Wavelet Scattering Transform, WST）的$m$模比构建的抗偏置统计量$R^{\rm wst}$，可在无需显式示踪粒子偏置建模的情况下实现精确宇宙学参数推断。该方法在\texttt{Kun}和\texttt{JiuTian}模拟中得到验证，其高斯过程回归模拟器达到百分之一量级的精度，对$Ω_m$、$σ_8$、$n_s$和$w_0$给出无偏约束，并将$Ω_m$--$σ_8$简并打破能力较两点相关函数（2PCF）提升约两倍，且在多种偏置情形下保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conventional large-scale structure statistics like the two-point correlation function (2PCF) capture limited non-Gaussian information and require explicit modeling of galaxy bias, which introduces systematic errors if the bias model is misspecified. This limits their robustness for precision cosmology with Stage-IV surveys, motivating the search for statistics that are inherently insensitive to tracer bias while retaining high cosmological information content.
\n\n
传统的宇宙大尺度结构统计量（如两点相关函数，2PCF）仅能捕获有限的非高斯信息，且需显式建模星系偏置（tracer bias），若偏置模型设定不当会引入系统误差。这限制了其在第四阶段（Stage-IV）巡天中进行精确宇宙学分析的稳健性，因此亟需发展一类对示踪粒子偏置不敏感但又能保留丰富宇宙学信息的新型统计量。

Method: The authors construct $R^{\rm wst}$ from ratios of $m$-modes in the wavelet scattering transform (WST), which inherently suppresses dependence on linear and nonlinear bias by design. They employ simulation-based inference: training a Gaussian-process-regression emulator on the \texttt{Kun} N-body simulation suite to predict $R^{\rm wst}$ as a function of cosmological parameters ($Ω_m$, $σ_8$, $n_s$, $w_0$), and using the \texttt{JiuTian} simulations to estimate the covariance matrix and validate performance across different bias models.
\n\n
作者基于小波散射变换（WST）中的$m$模比构造统计量$R^{\rm wst}$，其设计本身可有效抑制对线性和非线性偏置的依赖。他们采用基于模拟的推断方法：利用\texttt{Kun} N体模拟数据集训练高斯过程回归（Gaussian-process regression）模拟器，以预测$R^{\rm wst}$作为宇宙学参数（$Ω_m$、$σ_8$、$n_s$、$w_0$）的函数；并使用\texttt{JiuTian}模拟估算协方差矩阵，并在不同偏置模型下验证其性能。

Result: The emulator achieves percent-level accuracy in predicting $R^{\rm wst}$. Cosmological inference using $R^{\rm wst}$ yields unbiased constraints on $Ω_m$, $σ_8$, $n_s$, and $w_0$, and breaks the $Ω_m$--$σ_8$ degeneracy approximately twice as effectively as the 2PCF. Crucially, these results remain stable across a wide range of tracer-bias prescriptions, confirming that $R^{\rm wst}$ mitigates bias-induced systematics without requiring explicit bias modeling.
\n\n
模拟器在预测$R^{\rm wst}$时达到百分之一量级的精度。使用$R^{\rm wst}$进行宇宙学参数推断可对$Ω_m$、$σ_8$、$n_s$和$w_0$给出无偏约束，且将$Ω_m$--$σ_8$简并的打破能力提升至2PCF的约两倍。关键的是，这些结果在广泛的示踪粒子偏置设定下均保持稳定，证实了$R^{\rm wst}$无需显式偏置建模即可有效缓解偏置引起的系统误差。

Conclusion: $R^{\rm wst}$ is a powerful, bias-robust summary statistic that captures non-Gaussian information from large-scale structure while avoiding the need for explicit bias modeling. Its stability and enhanced constraining power make it highly suitable for precision cosmology with upcoming Stage-IV galaxy surveys such as Euclid, Rubin Observatory, and SPHEREx.
\n\n
$R^{\rm wst}$是一种强大且抗偏置的汇总统计量，能够从大尺度结构中提取非高斯信息，同时避免显式偏置建模的需求。其稳健性和更强的约束能力使其非常适合用于欧几里得（Euclid）、鲁宾天文台（Rubin Observatory）和SPHEREx等即将开展的第四阶段（Stage-IV）星系巡天的精确宇宙学分析。

Abstract: A central challenge in precision cosmology with galaxy surveys is to extract non-Gaussian information from large-scale structure while controlling systematic uncertainties such as tracer bias. Conventional clustering statistics, such as the two-point correlation function (2PCF), capture limited nonlinear information and typically require explicit bias modeling, which can introduce systematic errors if the adopted bias prescription is inaccurate. To address this problem, we introduce $R^{\rm wst}$, a bias-robust statistic constructed from $m$-mode ratios of the wavelet scattering transform (WST). Using simulation-based inference, we train a Gaussian-process-regression emulator on the \texttt{Kun} simulation suite and use \texttt{JiuTian} simulations for covariance estimation and validation. The emulator achieves percent-level accuracy, sufficient for the expected observational uncertainties. We show that $R^{\rm wst}$ yields unbiased constraints on $Ω_m$, $σ_8$, $n_s$, and $w_0$, and improves the breaking of the $Ω_m$--$σ_8$ degeneracy by about a factor of two compared with 2PCF. Its constraining power remains stable across a broad range of tracer-bias scenarios, demonstrating that $R^{\rm wst}$ can mitigate bias-induced systematics without explicit bias modeling. These results establish $R^{\rm wst}$ as a powerful and robust statistic for precision cosmology with Stage-IV surveys.

Method: Joint analysis of six-year DES data including Type Ia supernovae (SN), baryon acoustic oscillations (BAO), and 3×2pt (combined cosmic shear, galaxy-galaxy lensing, and galaxy clustering). Robustness is tested by systematically excluding individual probes and adding external datasets (DESI DR2 BAO, Planck CMB).

联合分析DES六年数据，包括Ia型超新星（SN）、重子声学振荡（BAO）和3×2pt（宇宙剪切、星系-星系透镜和星系成团性的组合）。分析采用平坦的 $w_0w_a$CDM 宇宙学模型，其中 $w(a) = w_0 + w_a(1 - a)$。通过马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）采样后验分布，结合几何探针（SN、BAO）与结构增长探针（3×2pt）进行约束。通过系统性地剔除单个探针并加入外部数据集（DESI DR2 BAO、Planck CMB）来检验结果的稳健性。

Including Planck CMB gives $w_0 = -0.82^{+0.05}_{-0.05}$, $w_a = -0.63^{+0.21}_{-0.18}$ ($3.0σ$). Excluding SN still gives $2.6σ$ deviation, confirming robustness. All combinations consistently find $w_0 > -1$ and $w_a < 0$.

Abstract: We present results on dark energy evolution, assuming a time-dependent equation of state $w(a)=w_0+w_a(1-a)$, from growth and geometric probes using the full six-year Dark Energy Survey dataset: type Ia supernovae, baryon acoustic oscillations, and weak gravitational lensing and galaxy clustering (3$\times$2pt). The combination yields $w_0=-0.84^{+0.10}_{-0.10}$ and $w_a=-0.44^{+0.60}_{-0.55}$, the tightest constraints ever obtained from a single survey, with $2.2σ$ deviation from a cosmological constant. Adding the DESI DR2 BAO data yields $w_0=-0.84^{+0.06}_{-0.07}$ and $w_a=-0.53^{+0.33}_{-0.28}$, representing the most stringent low-redshift-only test of dynamical dark energy to date, with a $2.3σ$ deviation. In this combination, adding 3$\times$2pt doubles the constraining power. Finally, when combined with primary CMB information, we obtain $w_0=-0.82^{+0.05}_{-0.05}$, $w_a=-0.63^{+0.21}_{-0.18}$, with a $3.0σ$ deviation. We find that including 3$\times$2pt in the previously studied SN + DESI BAO + CMB combination leaves the significance essentially unchanged ($3.2 σ$ to $3.0σ$) while improving the figure of merit by $\sim$10\%. We systematically investigate the impact of leaving out each one of the probes and find that the significance of the deviation from a cosmological constant ranges from 2.3 to 3.2$σ$, with best-fit parameters consistently in the region $w_0 >-1$ and $w_a <0$. Excluding SN from the all data combination yields a $2.6σ$ departure from $Λ$CDM, providing a cross-check independent of supernova photometric calibration. These results support the weak preference for evolving dark energy reported by several recent cosmological analyses. By combining growth and geometric probes from a single survey, this work realizes the multi-probe dark energy program envisioned at the inception of DES.

### [7] [GWTC-5.0: Constraints on the Cosmic Expansion Rate and Modified Gravitational-wave Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27227)
*The LIGO Scientific Collaboration,the Virgo Collaboration,the KAGRA Collaboration*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper uses 236 gravitational-wave events from GWTC-5.0 to measure the Hubble constant $H_0$, combining mass-spectrum features, statistical galaxy association, and the standard siren GW170817. The result is $H_0 = {71.0}_{-7.1}^{+9.0}\,\text{km}\,\text{s}^{-1}\,\text{Mpc}^{-1}$, with a 25.7% reduction in uncertainty compared to GWTC-4.0. No deviations from general relativity in GW propagation are detected.

本文利用GWTC-5.0中的236个引力波事件，结合质量谱特征、统计星系关联以及标准汽笛事件GW170817，测量哈勃常数$H_0$，得到$H_0 = {71.0}_{-7.1}^{+9.0}\,\text{km}\,\text{s}^{-1}\,\text{Mpc}^{-1}$，相比GWTC-4.0不确定性降低25.7%。未发现引力波传播中偏离广义相对论（GR）的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to provide an independent and increasingly precise measurement of the Hubble constant $H_0$ using gravitational waves as standard sirens, thereby helping to resolve the tension between early- and late-Universe determinations of $H_0$. Additionally, the study aims to test general relativity by constraining modifications to gravitational-wave propagation that would alter the inferred luminosity distance.
\n\n
该研究旨在利用引力波作为标准汽笛（standard sirens），对哈勃常数$H_0$进行独立且日益精确的测量，以帮助解决早期宇宙与晚期宇宙对$H_0$测定之间的张力。此外，通过限制会改变引力波光度距离推断的传播修正，检验广义相对论（GR）。

Method: The authors analyze 236 GW sources from GWTC-5.0. They infer redshifts via two methods: i) features in the source-frame mass spectrum (e.g., the "mass gap" or peaks), and ii) statistical cross-matching with the DES-Y6 galaxy catalog. Luminosity distances come directly from GW waveform modeling. Combining these yields a posterior on $H_0$, incorporating prior information from GW170817. They also perform parameterized tests of GR by allowing for deviations in the GW luminosity distance–redshift relation.
\n\n
作者分析了GWTC-5.0中的236个引力波源。他们通过两种方法推断红移：i) 源静止系质量谱中的特征（如“质量间隙”或峰值），ii) 与DES-Y6星系目录的统计交叉匹配。光度距离直接来自引力波波形建模。结合这些信息，并纳入GW170817的先验信息，得到$H_0$的后验分布。他们还通过允许引力波光度距离–红移关系偏离标准形式，进行参数化的广义相对论（GR）检验。

Result: The analysis yields $H_0 = {71.0}_{-7.1}^{+9.0}\,\text{km}\,\text{s}^{-1}\,\text{Mpc}^{-1}$ (68% credible interval), improving the precision by 25.7% over GWTC-4.0 due to more events and better sky localization. The reconstructed source-frame mass distribution has reduced uncertainties. Parameterized tests of GW propagation show no statistically significant deviations from general relativity.
\n\n
分析得出$H_0 = {71.0}_{-7.1}^{+9.0}\,\text{km}\,\text{s}^{-1}\,\text{Mpc}^{-1}$（68%可信区间），相比GWTC-4.0因更多事件和更优天区定位而将精度提升25.7%。重建的源静止系质量分布不确定性降低。对引力波传播的参数化检验未发现广义相对论（GR）的显著偏离。

Conclusion: Gravitational-wave standard sirens continue to provide competitive and independent constraints on $H_0$, with precision steadily improving as catalogs grow and sky localization improves.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Observationally relevant SIGW require large curvature perturbations at small scales, which arise in inflationary models that deviate from slow-roll and produce strongly non-Gaussian statistics. 具有观测意义的 SIGW 要求在小尺度上产生大的曲率扰动，这源于偏离慢滚（slow-roll）的暴胀模型，并导致强烈的非高斯统计。现有方法难以超越微扰或高斯近似来计算由此产生的 SIGW 谱，因为所需的傅里叶空间积分在计算上难以处理。

Method: The authors solve the second-order wave equation semi-analytically, yielding an expression for the SIGW power spectrum involving multidimensional Fourier integrals. These integrals are recast as a sum of approximately 50 spatial convolutions, each computable via Fast Fourier Transform (FFT). The final SIGW power spectrum is then measured directly from lattice realizations using this convolution decomposition. The entire pipeline is implemented in FLAN-SIGW, a GPU-accelerated code.
\n\n
作者半解析地求解二阶波动方程，得到涉及多维傅里叶积分的 SIGW 功率谱表达式。这些积分被重构为约 50 个空间卷积之和，每个卷积均可通过快速傅里叶变换（FFT）计算。最终的 SIGW 功率谱通过该卷积分解直接从格点实现中测量。整个流程在 GPU 加速代码 FLAN-SIGW 中实现。

Result: The method enables computation of fully non-perturbative, non-Gaussian SIGW spectra in seconds with modest computational resources and achieves an error of about 10%. The implementation assumes a radiation-dominated background with equation-of-state parameter $w = 1/3$, serving as a benchmark for code validation.

该方法能够在普通计算资源下数秒内计算完全非微扰、非高斯的 SIGW 谱，误差约为 10%。实现中假设了状态方程参数为 $w = 1/3$ 的辐射主导背景，作为代码验证的基准。

Conclusion: The paper presents an efficient, publicly available tool (FLAN-SIGW) that overcomes previous computational bottlenecks in SIGW calculations by leveraging convolution decomposition and FFTs. This opens the door to rapid exploration of non-Gaussian inflationary models and their SIGW signatures, crucial for interpreting future gravitational-wave observations.

Abstract: Scalar Induced Gravitational Waves (SIGW) are generated at second order in perturbation theory and to achieve observational relevance, inflationary dynamics must evade the standard slow-roll scenario at small scales, generating large curvature perturbations following strongly non-Gaussian statistics. We propose a method to efficiently compute the SIGW spectrum including arbitrary non-Gaussianities. First, we solve the wave equation adopting semi-analytic methods; this results in an expression involving integrals in Fourier space which are impossible to solve directly on a lattice. We overcome this bottleneck by recasting these integrals as a sum of about 50 convolutions, each of which can be computed efficiently with FFT methods. Finally, the power spectrum is measured directly from the lattice realization. We implement this in FLAN-SIGW, a GPU-accelerated code capable of computing fully non-perturbative, non-Gaussian SIGW spectra in seconds with an error within 10% with modest computational resources. The code is made public at https://github.com/giovannipiccoli99/FLAN-SIGW. In this first implementation, in order to assess the performance of the method, we adopt a standard radiation-dominated background with $w = 1/3$.

The analysis is independent of specific data sets and focuses on logical consistency and physical interpretation.

Conclusion: Regardless of whether current observational preferences for dynamical dark energy persist, it is crucial to distinguish between effective phenomenological descriptions and fundamental physics. Abstract: In observational cosmology, the dark energy density and equation of state are effective quantities reconstructed at the background level under a set of assumptions. These include the FLRW framework, the standard Friedmann equation of General Relativity, and separately conserved non-relativistic matter at late times. Recent analyses involving DESI BAO measurements combined with CMB and supernova data have shown mild preference for dynamical dark energy featuring phantom or phantom-crossing behaviour. While the statistical significance of these trends remains limited, and unresolved systematics or modelling uncertainties may still be important, the resulting discussions have highlighted the need for a clearer interpretation of effective dark energy reconstruction. In particular, effective phantom behaviour does not necessarily imply the existence of a fundamental phantom field, microscopic ghost instabilities, violation of the null energy condition by the fundamental stress tensor, or a catastrophic cosmic future. The purpose of this work is to clarify these distinctions explicitly and systematically, independently of whether the current observational preference for dynamical dark energy survives future data. We discuss the definition of effective dark energy in cosmology, the interpretation of phantom and phantom-crossing behaviour, and physical mechanisms through which effective phantom behaviour may arise without fundamental pathologies. While many of these distinctions are familiar within the dark energy reconstruction community, they are often left implicit in broader discussions of dynamical dark energy. We hope that this work will remain useful beyond the present observational situation as a clarification of what observationally reconstructed dark energy does and does not imply.

While fiducial models are accurately recovered, background-only measurements lack decisive model discrimination; however, derivative-sensitive diagnostics identify optimal redshift windows ($z\simeq1.6–1.8$ for ET, $z\simeq2.6–2.9$ for LISA) for distinguishing theories. This work establishes a Bayesian, nonparametric GPR framework to locate where cosmological information is most informative.

未来的LISA和爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope）引力波标准汽笛（standard siren）星表将探测传统电磁观测难以触及的高红移宇宙膨胀历史。作者采用高斯过程回归（Gaussian Process Regression, GPR）在六种宇宙学模型（$Λ$CDM、CPL、CPL+$Λ$、相互作用暗物质、相互作用暗能量、轴子启发的早期暗能量）下重构共动距离及其导数，并传播完整的GP协方差（包括导数交叉协方差）以稳健地计算哈勃参数 $H(z)$ 及其他诊断量如减速度参数 $q(z)$、物质密度参数 $\mathcal{O}_{m}(z)$、总状态方程 $w_{\rm total}(z)$ 和曲率参数 $κ(z)$。虽然标准汽笛能准确复现基准膨胀历史，但仅靠背景测量无法在统计上明确区分不同模型；然而，对导数敏感的诊断量识别出最佳区分红移窗口（ET为 $z\simeq1.6–1.8$，LISA为 $z\simeq2.6–2.9$）。该工作建立了一个贝叶斯、非参数化的GPR框架，用于精准定位最具信息量的宇宙学红移区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current electromagnetic probes of cosmic expansion are limited at high redshifts ($z \gtrsim 2$), creating a gap in our understanding of late-time cosmology. Gravitational wave standard sirens offer a complementary, absolute distance measurement independent of the cosmic distance ladder. However, it remains unclear how effectively such data can distinguish between competing cosmological models that predict similar background expansions. The paper aims to develop a model-independent method to assess the discriminatory power of future GW catalogs by focusing on reconstructed kinematic quantities and their uncertainties, thereby identifying where in redshift space the most decisive cosmological information resides.
\n\n
当前电磁手段对宇宙膨胀的探测在高红移（$z \gtrsim 2$）处受限，导致我们对晚期宇宙学的理解存在空白。引力波标准汽笛提供了一种不依赖宇宙距离阶梯的绝对距离测量，可作为互补探针。然而，尚不清楚此类数据在多大程度上能区分那些预测相似背景膨胀历史的竞争性宇宙学模型。本文旨在发展一种模型无关的方法，通过重构运动学量（如 $H(z)$、$q(z)$ 等）并完整传播其不确定性，评估未来引力波星表的模型区分能力，从而识别出最具判别力的红移区间。

Method: The authors employ Gaussian Process Regression (GPR)—a Bayesian nonparametric technique—to reconstruct the comoving distance $D_C(z)$ from simulated standard siren catalogs for LISA and Einstein Telescope under six fiducial cosmologies. Crucially, they compute not only the mean and variance of $D_C(z)$ but also its first and second derivatives by analytically propagating the full GP covariance matrix, including cross-covariances between function values and derivatives. From these, they derive key cosmographic diagnostics: $H(z) = c / D_C'(z)$, deceleration parameter $q(z)$, effective matter density $\mathcal{O}_{m}(z)$, total equation of state $w_{\rm total}(z)$, and spatial curvature $κ(z)$. Model discrimination is quantified using the pointwise marginal Hellinger distance between reconstructed distributions of these diagnostics across different fiducial models.

作者采用高斯过程回归（Gaussian Process Regression, GPR）——一种贝叶斯非参数方法——基于LISA和爱因斯坦望远镜的模拟标准汽笛星表，在六种基准宇宙学模型下重构共动距离 $D_C(z)$。关键创新在于不仅计算 $D_C(z)$ 的均值与方差，还通过解析传播完整的GP协方差矩阵（包括函数值与导数之间的交叉协方差）来获得其一阶和二阶导数。由此推导出关键宇宙学诊断量：哈勃参数 $H(z) = c / D_C'(z)$、减速度参数 $q(z)$、有效物质密度 $\mathcal{O}_{m}(z)$、总状态方程 $w_{\rm total}(z)$ 以及空间曲率 $κ(z)$。模型区分能力通过这些诊断量在不同基准模型间重构分布的逐点边缘Hellinger距离进行量化。

Result: The GPR reconstruction accurately recovers the input fiducial expansion histories for all six models from both LISA and ET mock catalogs. However, when comparing the reconstructed background quantities (e.g., $D_C(z)$ or $H(z)$) alone, the Hellinger distance analysis shows insufficient statistical separation to decisively favor one model over another. In contrast, derivative-sensitive diagnostics—particularly those involving higher-order derivatives like $q(z)$ or $w_{\rm total}(z)$—exhibit pronounced differences in specific redshift intervals. For Einstein Telescope, maximal discrimination occurs at $z \simeq 1.6–1.8$; for LISA, at $z \simeq 2.6–2.9$. These windows correspond to epochs where model predictions diverge most significantly in their kinematic behavior.
\n\n
GPR重构能从LISA和爱因斯坦望远镜的模拟星表中准确复现全部六种基准模型的输入膨胀历史。然而，仅比较重构的背景量（如 $D_C(z)$ 或 $H(z)$）时，Hellinger距离分析表明模型间缺乏足够的统计区分度以明确支持某一特定模型。相比之下，对导数敏感的诊断量——尤其是涉及高阶导数的量如 $q(z)$ 或 $w_{\rm total}(z)$——在特定红移区间表现出显著差异。对于爱因斯坦望远镜，最大区分能力出现在 $z \simeq 1.6–1.8$；对于LISA，则在 $z \simeq 2.6–2.9$。这些红移窗口对应于不同模型在运动学行为上分歧最大的时期。

Conclusion: Gravitational wave standard sirens are powerful tools for reconstructing cosmic expansion, but their ability to discriminate between viable cosmological models hinges not on the background distance alone, but on precise measurements of its derivatives. The proposed Bayesian GPR framework provides a rigorous, nonparametric method to propagate uncertainties and identify optimal redshift ranges where future GW observations will deliver maximal model-discriminatory power. This approach highlights the importance of derivative-sensitive cosmographic diagnostics and offers a machine learning-enhanced strategy for guiding observational priorities in next-generation GW cosmology.
\n\n
引力波标准汽笛是重构宇宙膨胀历史的有力工具，但其区分可行宇宙学模型的能力并不依赖于背景距离本身，而在于对其导数的精确测量。所提出的贝叶斯GPR框架提供了一种严格且非参数化的方法，可传播不确定性并识别出未来引力波观测最具模型区分力的最佳红移范围。该方法凸显了对导数敏感的宇宙学诊断量的重要性，并为下一代引力波宇宙学中的观测优先级设定提供了融合机器学习的策略。

Abstract: Future gravitational wave (GW) standard siren catalogues will probe the late-time expansion history of the Universe across redshift ranges largely inaccessible to traditional electromagnetic observations. To determine how effectively this background distance information can distinguish between viable cosmological models, we introduce a model-independent reconstruction framework utilizing Gaussian Process Regression (GPR). Analyzing mock LISA and Einstein Telescope (ET) catalogues across six fiducial cosmological backgrounds-$Λ$CDM, CPL, CPL+$Λ$, interacting dark matter, interacting dark energy and axion inspired early dark energy. We reconstruct the comoving distance and its derivatives. Crucially, we propagated the full GP covariance, including derivative cross-covariances, to robustly evaluate the Hubble parameter $H(z)$ and other diagnostics such as $q(z)$, $\mathcal{O}_{m}(z)$ $w_{\rm total}(z)$ and $κ(z)$. While our analysis demonstrates that GW bright standard sirens faithfully recover fiducial expansion histories, applying pointwise marginal Hellinger distance reveals that background measurements alone do not provide decisive statistical separation among models. Instead, derivative sensitive diagnostics pinpoint specific redshift windows (e.g., $z\simeq1.6-1.8$ for ET and $z\simeq2.6-2.9$ for LISA) where future catalogues will maximize their discriminatory power. As machine learning methodologies become increasingly integral to astrophysics and cosmology, this Bayesian GPR pipeline offers a principled, nonparametric approach to precisely identifying where the most valuable cosmological information lies.

</details>

Conclusion: Black holes in theories incorporating ModMax electrodynamics, Lorentz violation via Kalb-Ramond fields, and surrounding perfect-fluid dark matter exhibit rich geometric and thermodynamic structures distinct from Reissner–Nordström solutions. These features suggest that black holes in Lorentz-violating backgrounds surrounded by dark matter provide a useful arena for probing deviations from standard charged black-hole thermodynamics.

味真空的能量-动量张量满足状态方程 $p \\approx 0$，符合尘埃和冷暗物质的特征。在弱场区域，它产生形式为 $\\Phi(r) = -\\frac{GM}{r}(1 + \\alpha e^{-r/\\lambda})$ 的引力势，其中 $\\alpha$ 和 $\\lambda$ 依赖于中微子质量平方差和混合角。这种Yukawa修正势能导致与旋涡星系观测一致的渐近平坦旋转曲线。

Abstract: We review recent results showing that, within the framework of quantum field theory in curved spacetime, the semiclassical energy-momentum tensor of the neutrino flavor vacuum fulfills the equation of state of dust and cold dark matter. By considering spherically symmetric spacetimes in the weak field approximation, the flavor vacuum is shown to contribute as a Yukawa correction to the Newtonian potential. We discuss how this modified potential provides a mechanism to account for the flat rotation curves of spiral galaxies. ### [14] [Covariant Dynamical Systems Formulation of the Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26187)
*Eduardo Bittencourt,Mariam Campbell,Peter K. S. Dunsby,Sergio E. Jorás*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper reformulates the Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff (TOV) equations for static, spherically symmetric perfect-fluid stars in General Relativity using the $1+1+2$ semi-tetrad formalism, yielding a covariant first-order autonomous dynamical system. For a linear equation of state (EoS), this reduces to a planar phase space with interpretable equilibrium points; for polytropic or general EoS, it becomes a full three-dimensional flow. This approach provides a geometrically transparent phase-space description that encodes the standard metric formulation in covariant variables.
\n\n
本文利用 $1+1+2$ 半标架形式（semi-tetrad formalism）重新表述了广义相对论中静态球对称理想流体恒星的 Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff（TOV）方程，将其转化为协变的一阶自治动力系统。对于线性状态方程（EoS），该系统可进一步简化为二维相空间，其有限和渐近平衡点具有清晰的几何解释；而对于多方或更一般的 EoS，则呈现出真正的三维动力学结构。该方法不仅提供了紧凑、协变且物理透明的恒星结构描述，还阐明了标准度规表述如何通过几何意义明确的协变量嵌入全局相空间结构中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to provide a more geometrically intuitive and covariant phase-space formulation of relativistic stellar structure equations. Traditional approaches rely on metric components and coordinate-dependent variables, which obscure the underlying geometric and physical content. The $1+1+2$ semi-tetrad formalism offers a natural framework to describe locally rotationally symmetric (LRS) static spacetimes using fully covariant kinematic and dynamic variables, enabling a clearer connection between the TOV system and dynamical systems theory.
\n\n
动机在于为相对论恒星结构方程提供一种更具几何直观性和协变性的相空间表述。传统方法依赖于度规分量和坐标相关的变量，掩盖了其内在的几何与物理内容。$1+1+2$ 半标架形式为描述局部旋转对称（LRS）静态时空提供了一个自然框架，使用完全协变的运动学与动力学变量，从而更清晰地建立 TOV 系统与动力系统理论之间的联系。

Method: The authors employ the $1+1+2$ semi-tetrad covariant formalism to decompose spacetime geometry into scalar variables adapted to spherical symmetry. They rewrite the Einstein field equations and conservation laws as a first-order autonomous system in terms of dimensionless, normalized covariant variables. For a general equation of state $p = p(\\rho)$, this yields a three-dimensional dynamical system. In the special case of a linear EoS ($p = w\\rho$), the system decouples to a two-dimensional planar flow. Equilibrium points are identified and their stability analyzed via linearization, with physical interpretations tied directly to geometric quantities like energy density $\\rho$, pressure $p$, and gravitational potential.
\n\n
作者采用 $1+1+2$ 半标架协变形式，将时空几何分解为适应球对称性的标量变量，并将爱因斯坦场方程与守恒律重写为关于无量纲归一化协变量的一阶自治系统。对于一般状态方程 $p = p(\\rho)$，得到一个三维动力系统；在线性 EoS（$p = w\\rho$）的特殊情形下，系统退耦为二维平面流。通过线性化分析确定平衡点及其稳定性，并将这些点直接关联到能量密度 $\\rho$、压强 $p$ 和引力势等几何物理量。

Result: The TOV equations are successfully recast as an autonomous dynamical system in covariant phase space. For linear EoS, all finite and asymptotic equilibrium points are classified, with stable configurations corresponding to physically viable stellar models (e.g., regular centers, asymptotic flatness). The phase portrait reveals how different solution branches (e.g., interior Schwarzschild, singular solutions) emerge from the global flow structure. For polytropic EoS, the necessity of a third dimension in phase space is demonstrated, reflecting the nonlinearity of the EoS and enriching the dynamical behavior beyond planar systems.
\n\n
TOV 方程被成功重构为协变相空间中的自治动力系统。对于线性 EoS，所有有限与渐近平衡点均被分类，其中稳定构型对应物理上合理的恒星模型（如规则中心、渐近平直）。相图揭示了不同解分支（如内 Schwarzschild 解、奇异解）如何从全局流结构中自然涌现。对于多方 EoS，相空间必须引入第三维，反映了 EoS 的非线性特性，并展现出超越平面系统的更丰富动力学行为。

Conclusion: The $1+1+2$ semi-tetrad approach provides a powerful, geometrically transparent framework for analyzing relativistic stellar structure. It unifies the description of diverse stellar solutions within a single phase-space picture, clarifies the role of the equation of state in shaping the dynamics, and demonstrates how the standard metric-based TOV formulation is embedded in a covariant dynamical system. This formalism opens avenues for applying modern dynamical systems techniques to problems in relativistic astrophysics.
\n\n
$1+1+2$ 半标架方法为分析相对论恒星结构提供了一个强大而几何透明的框架。它将各类恒星解统一于单一相空间图像中，阐明了状态方程在塑造动力学行为中的作用，并展示了基于度规的标准 TOV 表述如何嵌入协变动力系统之中。该形式体系为将现代动力系统技术应用于相对论天体物理问题开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We revisit static, spherically symmetric perfect-fluid stellar models in General Relativity within the framework of the $1+1+2$ semi-tetrad formalism. For locally rotationally symmetric static spacetimes, the Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff system can be expressed as a covariant first-order dynamical system and, after suitable normalization, reformulated as a three-dimensional autonomous flow for a general equation of state (EoS). In the case of a linear EoS, the system reduces further to a planar dynamical system whose finite and asymptotic equilibrium points, together with their stability properties, admit a clear geometrical interpretation in terms of covariant variables. For more general equations of state, such as the polytropic case, the dynamics naturally acquire a genuinely three-dimensional character. Beyond providing a compact, covariant, and physically transparent reformulation of the relativistic stellar problem, the present analysis clarifies how the standard metric description is encoded within a global phase-space structure constructed from geometrically meaningful covariant variables.

For $\tilde{a} < \tilde{a}_c = 1/\sqrt{24}$, the system exhibits van der Waals–type phase behavior with three coexisting equilibria, and topological analysis reveals a total winding number $W=+1$, placing SV–AdS in the same universality class as other regular AdS black holes (e.g., Bardeen–AdS, Hayward–AdS), distinct from singular Schwarzschild–AdS ($W=0$).

Motivation: The motivation is to explore how singularity resolution via the Simpson–Visser regularization affects the holographic thermodynamics and phase structure of AdS black holes. 动机在于探索通过Simpson–Visser正则化解决奇点问题如何影响AdS黑洞的全息热力学和相结构。作者将正则化几何视为边界CFT的体对偶，旨在理解此类正则化是否保留或改变黑洞热力学的普适特征，特别是与奇异Schwarzschild–AdS及其他正则黑洞模型（如Bardeen–AdS）的对比。

Method: The authors employ the AdS/CFT correspondence, using the regularized lapse function $f(r)=1-2M/\sqrt{r^2+a^2}+(r^2+a^2)/\ell^2$ to define the SV–AdS geometry. 作者采用AdS/CFT对偶，利用正则化lapse函数$f(r)=1-2M/\sqrt{r^2+a^2}+(r^2+a^2)/\ell^2$定义SV–AdS几何。他们以视界半径和SV参数$a$为变量，解析计算全息热力学量——熵、能量、温度和化学势。通过离壳自由能分析相结构，并借助拓扑矢量场分析赋予局域绕数及总拓扑荷$W$，研究其拓扑性质。

当$\tilde{a} < \tilde{a}_c = 1/\sqrt{24}$时，SV–AdS黑洞展现出van der Waals型相变，存在三个共存平衡分支（小、中、大）。拓扑矢量场分析给出局域绕数$(+1,-1,+1)$和总拓扑荷$W=+1$，表明SV–AdS与Bardeen–AdS、Hayward–AdS等正则AdS黑洞属于同一普适类，区别于$W=0$的Schwarzschild–AdS。

Simpson–Visser正则化不仅消除了中心奇点，还赋予AdS黑洞非平凡的拓扑结构（$W=+1$），该结构主导其热力学相行为。这使SV–AdS明确归属于AdS中正则黑洞的普适类，表明奇点消除可导致经典解所不具备的全新热力学与拓扑特征。

Abstract: We study a Simpson--Visser regularization of the four-dimensional Schwarzschild--anti--de\,Sitter (SV--AdS) black hole, treated as the bulk dual of a planar conformal field theory (CFT) on the AdS boundary. For each, the implied area spectrum $A_n$ is derived by solving $S(A_{n}) - S(A_{n-1}) = k_B \ln 2$, and the asymptotic behavior of the relative spacing $\frac{A_n - A_{n-1}}{A_n}$ is analyzed as $n \to \infty$.
\n\n

In the modified Rényi case, the nonsingular branch ($\delta > 0$) also exhibits vanishing relative spacing, but the singular branch ($\delta < 0$) leads to a pole at finite $n$, implying a maximum black hole area. For modified Kaniadakis entropy, a small-$\kappa$ expansion shows that the relative area spacing approaches a non-zero constant as $n \to \infty$, meaning the spectrum remains effectively discrete even at macroscopic scales—a significant departure from standard expectations.

对于Bekenstein–Hawking熵，复现了标准的Bekenstein–Mukhanov面积谱，具有恒定间距。对于Barrow熵，有效面积量子化参数$\gamma_n$变为能级依赖，但相对间距在$n \to \infty$时仍趋于零。在修正Rényi熵情形中，非奇异分支（$\delta > 0$）同样表现出相对间距趋于零，而奇异分支（$\delta < 0$）则在有限$n$处出现极点，意味着存在最大黑洞面积。对于修正Kaniadakis熵，小$\kappa$展开表明，相对面积间距在$n \to \infty$时趋于一个非零常数，意味着即使在宏观尺度上谱仍保持有效离散——这显著偏离了标准预期。

Abstract: We investigate black hole area quantization by imposing Landauer's principle on the discrete entropy change between consecutive area levels. The elementary transition is identified with the entropy cost of erasing one bit of information, \(ΔS=k_B\ln 2\). The same discrete construction is then applied to generalized entropy functionals. 作者采用带有 Manko–Ruiz 参数 $C$ 的 Taub–NUT 度规，并通过 Wald 方法引入渐近均匀磁场 $B$，以保持时空对称性。他们通过施加约束 $x = \cos\theta = 0$ 和 $\dot{x} = 0$ 来研究“约束”圆轨道，有效冻结赤道面外的运动。在此假设下，他们从洛伦兹力修正的测地线方程出发，推导运动方程，求得角速度 $\Omega$，并通过边际稳定性条件 $d^2V_{\mathrm{eff}}/dr^2 = 0$ 确定 ISCO 位置。同时，他们也识别出完全自洽的锥形轨道（$x \neq 0$）所需满足的剩余角约束。

\n\n
主要结果包括：(1) 给出了真实锥形圆轨道必须满足的剩余角约束的闭式表达式；(2) 得到了 ISCO 半径 $r_{\mathrm{ISCO}}$ 关于磁场 $B$、粒子电荷 $q$ 和参数 $C$ 的解析表达式；(3) 证明增大 $|B|$ 会单调减小 $r_{\mathrm{ISCO}}$；(4) 顺行与逆行 ISCO 分支由 $q$ 的符号区分，且两者之间的大小顺序相反；(5) 参数 $C$ 仅在 ISCO 位移中贡献次领头阶项，证实其主要起正则化作用而非主导物理效应。

Conclusion: The paper concludes that while the Taub–NUT geometry generically forces charged orbits onto cones, constrained equatorial circular orbits provide a tractable approximation that captures the leading influence of the magnetic field on ISCO. The Manko–Ruiz parameter $C$ has negligible impact on ISCO at leading order, reinforcing its interpretation as a gauge-like degree of freedom related to the Misner string. The natural next step is to relax the equatorial constraint and solve for fully self-consistent conical circular orbits satisfying the derived angular condition.

本文得出结论：尽管 Taub–NUT 几何通常迫使带电轨道位于锥面上，但约束的赤道圆轨道提供了一种可行的近似方法，能够捕捉磁场对 ISCO 的主导影响。Manko–Ruiz 参数 $C$ 在领头阶对 ISCO 几乎无影响，进一步支持其作为与 Misner 弦相关的类规范自由度的解释。下一步自然的工作是放松赤道约束，求解满足所推导角约束的完全自洽锥形圆轨道。

Abstract: We study circular motion of charged test particles on the equatorial slice of a Taub--NUT black hole with Manko--Ruiz parameter $C$, immersed in a weak external magnetic field introduced via Wald's prescription. Because the Taub--NUT metric is not reflection-symmetric about the equator once $l\neq 0$, generic charged orbits lie on cones $x=\cosθ\neq 0$ rather than on the equatorial plane. We therefore analyse \emph{constrained} circular orbits obtained by imposing $x=\dot x=0$, and we exhibit in closed form the residual angular constraint that a fully self-consistent orbit would have to satisfy. Within this scope we derive the circularity and marginal-stability conditions and study how $B$ and $C$ shift the ISCO radius for prograde and retrograde branches. Increasing $B$ monotonically decreases $r_{\mathrm{ISCO}}$; the sign of the particle charge splits the two branches, with the ordering reversed between prograde and retrograde motion; and $C$ contributes only subleading corrections. The extension to self-consistent conical orbits is the natural direction for follow-up work.

Abstract: In this paper, we systematically study three classes of three-dimensional Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli (BTZ) black holes based on different gravitational frameworks and matter field structures: the Einstein-Maxwell BTZ, the F(R)-Maxwell BTZ, and the F(R)-phantom BTZ.Within the canonical and grand canonical ensemble frameworks, we construct the generalized free energy of these black hole systems and systematically analyze the asymptotic behavior of Hawking thermodynamics.The results indicate that both gravitational modifications and matter fields significantly influence black hole topology: the F(R) gravitational modification alters the intrinsic topological class of the black hole compared to the BTZ black hole under Einsteinian gravity, while different matter fields further modulate the topological classification results.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to understand how dark matter distributions—particularly non-standard profiles such as those from self-interacting scalar field models—affect strong gravitational lensing around black holes. Method: The authors reconstruct the spacetime metric numerically using the Einstein cluster formalism, which allows embedding a central black hole within a spherically symmetric dark matter halo (either self-interacting scalar field or NFW-type). They then compute the effective potential for null geodesics to determine the photon sphere radius $r_{\\text{ph}}$ and critical impact parameter $b_{\\text{cr}}$. Using these, they calculate strong-lensing observables: positions and separations of relativistic images, their magnifications, and especially time delays between successive images, focusing on M87* and Sgr A*.

作者采用Einstein cluster形式对时空度规进行数值重构，将中心黑洞嵌入球对称的暗物质晕中（包括自相互作用标量场晕和NFW型晕）。随后计算零测地线的有效势以确定光子球半径$r_{\\text{ph}}$和临界碰撞参数$b_{\\text{cr}}$，并基于此计算强透镜观测量：相对论性像的位置与间距、放大率，尤其是连续像之间的时间延迟，并聚焦于M87*和Sgr A*。

Result: The study finds that both scalar-field and NFW dark matter halos induce only minor corrections to standard Schwarzschild lensing observables—typically at the $\mathcal{O}(10^{-3})$ level or below—leading to strong degeneracy among models. However, time delays between higher-order relativistic images exhibit systematic, albeit small, deviations that scale with black hole mass, making them the most promising probe of halo-induced spacetime modifications for very massive black holes.

研究发现，无论是标量场还是NFW型暗物质晕，对标准Schwarzschild透镜观测量仅引入微小修正——通常在$\mathcal{O}(10^{-3})$量级或更低——导致模型间高度简并。然而，高阶相对论性像之间的时间延迟展现出系统性的（尽管微小的）偏差，且该偏差随黑洞质量增大而增强，使其成为探测超大质量黑洞周围晕所引起时空修正的最有前景的观测量。

Conclusion: While conventional strong-lensing signatures (e.g., image positions and magnifications) are remarkably robust against the presence of realistic dark matter halos, time-domain measurements—specifically time delays between relativistic images—offer enhanced sensitivity to the underlying dark matter distribution. 尽管传统的强透镜信号（如像的位置和放大率）对现实暗物质晕的存在表现出极强的鲁棒性，但时域测量——特别是相对论性像之间的时间延迟——对底层暗物质分布具有更高的灵敏度。未来对Sgr A*或M87*周围透镜耀发事件的高精度计时观测，有望打破模型简并，从而限制黑洞附近的暗物质模型。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore the strong gravitational lensing properties of black holes embedded in self-interacting scalar field dark matter halos, together with NFW-type configurations for comparison. The corresponding spacetime geometry is reconstructed numerically through the Einstein cluster formalism, allowing us to study how the surrounding dark matter distribution affects the propagation of photons near the black hole. We first analyze the effective function governing photon trajectories and calculate the corresponding photon sphere radius and critical impact parameter. We then investigate different strong-lensing observables, including relativistic Einstein rings, finite-order image positions, image separations, magnifications, and time delays, with particular attention to the supermassive black holes M87* and Sgr A*. Our results show that the considered halo configurations produce only small deviations with respect to the Schwarzschild case, typically at the level of $\mathcal{O}(10^{-3})$ or smaller, leading to a strong observational degeneracy among the models. Nevertheless, small but systematic differences remain present, especially in the time delay between relativistic images, which provides the clearest amplification of the halo-induced corrections for very massive black holes. These results suggest that, although standard strong-lensing observables remain highly robust against the considered halo environments, time-domain signatures may offer a more promising way to probe the effect of dark matter surrounding black holes.

Method: The paper surveys analytical methods developed since Christodoulou–Klainerman, including the use of null foliations, vector field method, weighted energy estimates, and carefully chosen gauge conditions (e.g., wave coordinates or maximal slicing). Abstract: The nonlinear stability of Minkowski spacetime has been one of the central achievements in the mathematical theory of general relativity and, more broadly, in the analysis of nonlinear geometric wave equations. Since the seminal work of Christodoulou-Klainerman, the problem has shaped fundamental advances in our understanding of decay, dispersion, and the intricate interplay between geometry and analysis in the Einstein vacuum equations.
  This survey presents an overview of the main ideas and techniques underlying the stability theory of Minkowski spacetime. We emphasize the role of decay assumptions, geometric foliations, energy identities, and gauge choices in the global analysis. Particular attention is devoted to exterior stability results, minimal decay regimes, and the borderline case, where the failure of spacetime integrability for nonlinear interactions reveals subtle threshold phenomena.

Abstract: We ask whether two classical time-machine geometries, the Ori (2005) compact-vacuum-core metric and the Ahmed (2018) four-dimensional generalisation of Misner space, remain admissible exact solutions when the gravitational sector is enlarged to the recently proposed $f(R,\mathcal{L}_{m},Φ,X)$ class, an extension of $f(R,\mathcal{L}_{m})$ that couples curvature, the matter Lagrangian density, a scalar field $Φ$, and its kinetic invariant $X = g^{μν}\nabla_μΦ\nabla_νΦ$. This yields a consistent equal-time commutator algebra for the canonical variables. They then construct mode solutions respecting the field equations and define the Boulware, Unruh, and Hartle-Hawking vacua via appropriate boundary conditions. The Hawking spectrum is derived using Bogoliubov transformations between in- and out-modes in the Unruh vacuum. Finally, the Proca condensate is computed numerically from the renormalized two-point correlation function $\langle A_\\mu(x) A_\\nu(x') \\rangle$ evaluated in all three vacua.

Abstract: We perform a first-principles canonical quantization of a massive vector field, often referred to as the Proca field, in a Schwarzschild spacetime background. Abstract: LIGO, Virgo, KAGRA, and GEO form a network of gravitational-wave observatories. Data and analysis results from this network are made publicly available through the Gravitational Wave Open Science Center (GWOSC). This paper describes open data from this network, including the addition of data from the second part of the fourth observing run (O4b) and selected periods from the preceding engineering run (ER16), which were collected from times spanning April 6th, 2024 to January 28th, 2025. The public data set includes calibrated strain time series for each instrument, data from additional channels used for noise subtraction and detector characterization, and new analysis data products in the online GWOSC release associated with version 5.0 of the Gravitational-Wave Transient Catalog.

Furthermore, it seeks to establish how matter couples to this GEM field and whether the resulting interactions respect the expected conservation laws and Ward identities.
\n\n
动机源于在Weyl几何框架下，将Gravitoelectromagnetism (GEM) 表述为基于对称张量场 $A_{\mu\nu}$ 的规范理论时，厘清其规范结构与物理内容。不同于标准线性化广义相对论，该方法假设了一种更一般的规范对称性，可能包含额外自由度。本文旨在通过传播子分解来分离真实的动力学内容，理解在与守恒源耦合时哪些自旋分量是物理的，并确定与底层对称性兼容的一致规范固定方案。此外，还试图阐明物质如何与该GEM场耦合，以及所得相互作用是否满足预期的守恒律和Ward恒等式。

Method: The authors employ the Barnes–Rivers decomposition formalism to analyze the propagator of the symmetric tensor field $A_{\mu\nu}$, separating it into irreducible representations corresponding to spin-2, spin-1, and spin-0 sectors. They impose a restricted gauge symmetry $\\delta A_{\mu\nu} = \\partial_\mu \\xi_\nu + \\partial_\nu \\xi_\mu - \\frac{2}{D} \\eta_{\mu\nu} \\partial \\cdot \\xi$ (with $D$ spacetime dimensions), which differs from full diffeomorphism invariance. Physical relevance is assessed by coupling $A_{\mu\nu}$ to conserved external sources $T^{\\mu\nu}$ satisfying $\\partial_\\mu T^{\\mu\nu} = 0$. Two gauge-fixing conditions are examined: a Lorentz-like condition $\\partial^\\mu A_{\\mu\nu} - \\frac{1}{D} \\partial_\\nu A = 0$ and the de Donder condition $\\partial^\\mu \\bar{A}_{\\mu\nu} = 0$ (where $\\bar{A}_{\\mu\nu} = A_{\\mu\nu} - \\frac{1}{2} \\eta_{\\mu\nu} A$). The theory is then minimally coupled to fermionic and electromagnetic fields via diffeomorphism-like transformations, and the resulting Noether currents are computed. Finally, Ward identities are explicitly verified at the level of correlation functions to test gauge invariance of interaction vertices.
\n\n
作者采用Barnes–Rivers分解形式分析对称张量场 $A_{\mu\nu}$ 的传播子，将其分解为对应于自旋2、自旋1和自旋0的不可约表示。他们施加一种受限规范对称性 $\\delta A_{\\mu\\nu} = \\partial_\\mu \\xi_\\nu + \\partial_\\nu \\xi_\\mu - \\frac{2}{D} \\eta_{\\mu\\nu} \\partial \\cdot \\xi$（其中 $D$ 为时空维数），该对称性不同于完整的微分同胚不变性。通过将 $A_{\\mu\\nu}$ 与满足 $\\partial_\\mu T^{\\mu\\nu} = 0$ 的守恒外源 $T^{\\mu\\nu}$ 耦合来评估物理相关性。考察了两种规范固定条件：Lorentz型条件 $\\partial^\\mu A_{\\mu\\nu} - \\frac{1}{D} \\partial_\\nu A = 0$ 和de Donder条件 $\\partial^\\mu \\bar{A}_{\\mu\\nu} = 0$（其中 $\\bar{A}_{\\mu\\nu} = A_{\\mu\\nu} - \\frac{1}{2} \\eta_{\\mu\\nu} A$）。随后通过类微分同胚变换将该理论与费米子场和电磁场进行最小耦合，并计算相应的Noether流。最后，在关联函数层面上显式验证Ward恒等式，以检验相互作用顶点的规范不变性。

Result: The key results are: (1) The full propagator of $A_{\mu\nu}$ contains spin-2, spin-1, and spin-0 parts, but upon coupling to conserved sources ($\\partial_\\mu T^{\\mu\\nu}=0$), the spin-1 sector decouples completely, leaving only spin-2 and scalar contributions. (2) The resulting effective propagator takes the compact form $$\\langle A_{\\mu\\nu}(k) A_{\\rho\\sigma}(-k) \\rangle_{\\text{eff}} \\propto \\frac{1}{k^2} \\left( P^{(2)}_{\\mu\\nu,\\rho\\sigma} + c \\, P^{(0)}_{\\mu\\nu,\\rho\\sigma} \\right),$$ where $P^{(2)}$ and $P^{(0)}$ are Barnes–Rivers projectors for spin-2 and spin-0, resembling the linearized GR graviton propagator when $c=0$. (3) The Lorentz-like gauge condition is compatible with the restricted gauge symmetry and yields gauge-independent physical amplitudes, whereas the de Donder gauge introduces residual scalar modes that violate gauge independence. (4) Coupling to fermions and photons via diffeomorphism-like transformations yields conserved currents identical to their respective energy-momentum tensors $T^{\\mu\\nu}_{\\text{fermion}}$ and $T^{\\mu\\nu}_{\\text{EM}}$. (5) The associated Ward identities hold exactly, confirming the internal consistency of the gauge structure and interaction vertices.
\n\n
主要结果包括：(1) $A_{\\mu\\nu}$ 的完整传播子包含自旋2、自旋1和自旋0部分，但在与守恒源（$\\partial_\\mu T^{\\mu\\nu}=0$）耦合时，自旋1部分完全解耦，仅剩自旋2和标量贡献。(2) 所得有效传播子具有紧凑形式 $$\\langle A_{\\mu\\nu}(k) A_{\\rho\\sigma}(-k) \\rangle_{\\text{eff}} \\propto \\frac{1}{k^2} \\left( P^{(2)}_{\\mu\\nu,\\rho\\sigma} + c \\, P^{(0)}_{\\mu\\nu,\\rho\\sigma} \\right),$$ 其中 $P^{(2)}$ 和 $P^{(0)}$ 是Barnes–Rivers自旋2和自旋0投影算符；当 $c=0$ 时，该形式与线性化GR的引力子传播子一致。(3) Lorentz型规范条件与受限规范对称性兼容，并给出规范无关的物理振幅；而de Donder规范引入了破坏规范无关性的残余标量模。(4) 通过类微分同胚变换与费米子和光子耦合，所得守恒流恰好等于各自的能量-动量张量 $T^{\\mu\\nu}_{\\text{fermion}}$ 和 $T^{\\mu\\nu}_{\\text{EM}}$。(5) 相应的Ward恒等式严格成立，证实了规范结构和相互作用顶点的内部一致性。

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates that Gravitoelectromagnetism formulated via a symmetric tensor field with restricted Weyl-compatible gauge symmetry reproduces key features of linearized gravity: only spin-2 and scalar modes couple to conserved matter sources, and the effective interaction is mediated by a propagator structurally analogous to that of the graviton. Crucially, consistency requires gauge-fixing procedures that respect the underlying symmetry—specifically, the Lorentz-like condition—not the standard de Donder gauge. The equivalence of matter couplings to energy-momentum tensors and the validity of Ward identities confirm that this GEM framework provides a consistent, gauge-invariant effective field theory that embeds gravitoelectromagnetic phenomena within a broader geometric structure while maintaining compatibility with fundamental principles of gravitational interaction.
\n\n
本文表明，在受限Weyl兼容规范对称性下通过一个对称张量场构建的Gravitoelectromagnetism (GEM) 理论再现了线性化引力的关键特征：仅有自旋2和标量模与守恒物质源耦合，且有效相互作用由结构上类似于引力子传播子的传播子所介导。关键在于，一致性要求规范固定程序必须尊重底层对称性——特别是Lorentz型条件，而非标准的de Donder规范。物质耦合等价于能量-动量张量，以及Ward恒等式的成立，共同证实该GEM框架提供了一个自洽的、规范不变的有效场论，它将gravitoelectromagnetic现象嵌入更广泛的几何结构中，同时保持与引力相互作用基本原理的兼容性。

Abstract: Gravitoelectromagnetism in the Weyl formalism is investigated through an analysis of the consequences of a restricted gauge symmetry acting on the tensor field $A_{μν}$. The propagator associated with the GEM field is explicitly derived and decomposed within the Barnes--Rivers formalism, revealing contributions from the spin-2, spin-1, and scalar spin-0 sectors. By coupling the theory to conserved sources, it is shown that only the spin-2 and scalar sectors contribute to physical processes, while the spin-1 component decouples. The resulting effective propagator can then be written in a compact metric form closely resembling the graviton propagator of linearized General Relativity. The role of gauge fixing is also analyzed by considering both Lorentz-like and de Donder-type gauge conditions. It is shown that the Lorentz-like gauge is consistent with the restricted gauge symmetry of the theory and leads to gauge-independent physical amplitudes, whereas the de Donder gauge introduces residual gauge-dependent scalar contributions, signaling an incompatibility with the underlying symmetry structure. The gauge symmetry is further extended to the fermionic and electromagnetic sectors through diffeomorphism-like transformations. In both cases, conserved currents are derived and shown to coincide with the corresponding energy-momentum tensors, implying that the GEM field couples to matter in the same manner as in linearized gravity. Finally, the associated Ward identities are verified, providing a nontrivial consistency check of the gauge structure and interaction vertices of the theory.

</details>

Zhu,Z. -H. Zhu,Z. Zhu,D. Z. Zieba,A. B. Zimmerman,L. Zimmermann,M. E. Zucker*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The GWTC-5.0 catalog, introduced in this paper, compiles over 300 gravitational-wave transients from binary black hole and neutron star mergers detected during the second half of the fourth LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA observing run up to January 28, 2025, significantly expanding the dataset for population studies and follow-up analyses.
\n\n
本文介绍的GWTC-5.0目录汇总了截至2025年1月28日在LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA第四次观测运行后半段中探测到的300多个来自双黑洞和中子星并合的引力波瞬变事件，显著扩展了用于天体物理种群研究和后续分析的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to present an updated and substantially expanded version of the Gravitational-Wave Transient Catalog (GWTC-5.0) that incorporates new detections from recent observing runs, thereby providing a comprehensive resource for the scientific community to study the properties and populations of compact binary coalescences through gravitational-wave astronomy.
\n\n
本文旨在发布一个更新且大幅扩展的引力波瞬变事件目录（GWTC-5.0），纳入近期观测运行中的新探测结果，从而为科学界提供一个全面资源，以通过引力波天文学研究致密双星并合事件的性质和种群特征。

Method: This paper serves as an overview introducing the GWTC-5.0 catalog and its accompanying suite of articles. It does not present a novel theoretical or computational method itself but references the methodologies documented in the companion papers, which include data analysis pipelines, statistical inference frameworks, and event validation procedures used by the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA Collaboration to identify and characterize gravitational-wave transients.
\n\n
本文作为一篇概述性文章，介绍了GWTC-5.0目录及其配套系列论文。它本身并未提出新的理论或计算方法，而是引用了合作组在配套论文中详述的方法论，包括数据分析流程、统计推断框架以及用于识别和刻画引力波瞬变事件的事件验证程序。

Result: GWTC-5.0 reports more than 300 confirmed gravitational-wave events from merging binary systems of black holes and neutron stars, greatly increasing the statistical sample available for population inference, tests of general relativity, and multi-messenger astrophysics.
\n\n
GWTC-5.0报告了超过300个来自黑洞和中子星并合双星系统的已确认引力波事件，极大增加了可用于种群推断、广义相对论检验和多信使天体物理学研究的统计样本。

Conclusion: The release of GWTC-5.0 represents a major milestone in gravitational-wave astronomy, offering an extensive catalog that enables robust statistical studies of compact binary populations and serves as a foundation for future theoretical and observational investigations into the nature of gravitational-wave sources.
\n\n
GWTC-5.0的发布标志着引力波天文学的一个重要里程碑，提供了一个广泛的目录，使得对致密双星种群进行稳健的统计研究成为可能，并为未来关于引力波源本质的理论与观测研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: The Gravitational-Wave Transient Catalog (GWTC) is a collection of short-duration (transient) gravitational-wave signals identified by the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA Collaboration in gravitational-wave data produced by the eponymous detectors. The catalog provides information about the identified candidates, such as the arrival time and amplitude of the signal and properties of the signal's source as inferred from the observational data. GWTC is the release of this dataset and version 5.0 extends the catalog to include observations made during the second part of the fourth LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA observing run up until 2025 January 28. This paper marks an introduction to a collection of articles related to this version of the catalog, GWTC-5.0. This update significantly increases the number of detected merging binary systems of black holes and neutron stars to over 300, enabling many follow-up studies toward understanding the gravitational-wave universe. The collection of articles accompanying the catalog provides documentation of the methods used to analyze the data, summaries of the catalog of events, observational measurements drawn from the population, and detailed discussions of selected candidates.

</details>


### [35] [GWTC-5.0: Methods for Identifying and Characterizing Gravitational-wave Transients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27224)
*The LIGO Scientific Collaboration,the Virgo Collaboration,the KAGRA Collaboration*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper describes the analysis methods used to produce GWTC-5.0, the fifth release of the Gravitational-Wave Transient Catalog, focusing on data from the second half of the fourth observing run (O4b) of LIGO, Virgo, and KAGRA. It covers signal modeling, transient detection, data quality assessment, parameter estimation, waveform model comparison, and handling of large-scale analysis outputs.
\n\n
本文描述了用于生成引力波暂现源星表第五版（GWTC-5.0）的分析方法，重点针对LIGO、Virgo和KAGRA第四次观测运行后半段（O4b）的数据。内容涵盖信号建模、暂现源探测、数据质量评估、参数估计、波形模型比对以及大规模分析结果的处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The increasing volume and complexity of gravitational-wave data from advanced detectors necessitate robust, scalable, and well-documented analysis pipelines to reliably identify and characterize transient signals such as compact binary coalescences. The motivation is to ensure consistent, reproducible, and comprehensive catalog production for GWTC-5.0, enabling accurate astrophysical inference and facilitating community-wide use of the data.
\n\n
随着先进探测器产生的引力波数据在数量和复杂性上不断增长，亟需稳健、可扩展且文档完备的分析流程，以可靠地识别和刻画致密双星并合等暂现信号。其动机在于确保GWTC-5.0星表生成的一致性、可复现性和全面性，从而支持精确的天体物理推断并促进整个科学界对数据的有效利用。

Method: The authors employ a suite of established and updated analysis techniques within the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA Collaboration framework. These include matched filtering with waveform models for compact binary coalescences, unmodeled burst searches, data quality vetoes to exclude non-astrophysical artifacts, Bayesian parameter estimation using samplers like Bilby or LALInference, and hierarchical procedures for false-alarm rate estimation and event significance assessment. The pipeline integrates multiple search algorithms and validation protocols tailored to O4b data characteristics.

Method: The authors construct three parametric forms of the Hubble parameter $H(z)$ derived from the field equations of $f(R, L_{m}) = \frac{R}{2} + L_{m}^\lambda$ gravity. Cosmological diagnostics—including the deceleration parameter $q(z)$, energy density $\rho(z)$, pressure $p(z)$, equation of state $w(z)$, energy conditions (NEC, WEC, SEC, DEC), statefinder pair $\{r,s\}$, and thermodynamic quantities like temperature and entropy density—are computed analytically or numerically from the best-fit $H(z)$.
