Abstract: We probe the cosmic expansion scenario within the framework of $f(R, L_{m})$ gravity by employing a well-motivated functional form of $f(R, L_{m}) = \frac{R}{2} + L_{m}^λ$. Specifically, we introduce three novel cosmological models formulated in terms of the redshift-dependent Hubble parameter $H(z)$, offering deeper insights into the underlying cosmic dynamics. The models are further utilized to investigate the expansion history of the universe and the evolution of several cosmological parameters. By using the Bayesian methods based on the $χ^{2}$-minimization technique, the median values of the model parameters are determined for the cosmic chronometer (CC) and joint (CC+Pantheon) datasets. A comprehensive study of the deceleration parameter, energy density, pressure and the equation of state parameter is carried out to understand the universe's evolution. Additionally, the validity of the energy conditions and the behavior of the statefinder diagnostic are thoroughly examined. Finally, the thermodynamic viability of the models is confirmed through the evolution of temperature and entropy density, and the estimated age of the universe further exemplifies good agreement with late-time astronomical observations.

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper reinterprets the lukewarm Reissner–Nordström–de Sitter black hole branch as a zero-dissipation critical thermal manifold in a two-horizon nonequilibrium system, with a divergent relaxation time at a critical horizon ratio $ρ_*$. This structure is captured via a Bragg–Williams functional and an Onsager–Machlup action.

本文将 lukewarm Reissner–Nordström–de Sitter 黑洞分支重新解释为一个双视界非平衡系统中的零耗散临界热力学流形，在临界视界比 $ρ_*$ 处弛豫时间发散。该结构通过 Bragg–Williams 泛函和 Onsager–Machlup 作用量得以刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand the thermodynamic nature of the lukewarm branch of Reissner–Nordström–de Sitter black holes beyond its geometric definition (equal temperatures at cosmological and event horizons), by embedding it within nonequilibrium thermodynamics and identifying it as a critical manifold with vanishing entropy production.
\n\n
动机在于超越 lukewarm 分支的几何定义（宇宙学视界与事件视界温度相等），将其嵌入非平衡热力学框架，理解其热力学本质，并将其识别为熵产生率为零的临界流形。

Method: The authors analyze the fixed-charge sector of four-dimensional Reissner–Nordström–de Sitter spacetime using nonequilibrium thermodynamics. They define an inter-horizon thermal affinity that governs entropy production, linearize the thermal mode around the lukewarm branch, and compute the exact relaxation coefficient $K_L(ρ)$. The critical behavior near $ρ_*$ is encoded in a minimal Bragg–Williams free energy functional and an Onsager–Machlup stochastic action for effective thermal trajectories.
\n\n
作者在四维 Reissner–Nordström–de Sitter 时空的固定电荷扇区中应用非平衡热力学方法。他们定义了一个控制熵产生的视界间热亲和势，围绕 lukewarm 分支对热模进行线性化，并计算精确的弛豫系数 $K_L(ρ)$。在 $ρ_*$ 附近的临界行为被编码进一个极小 Bragg–Williams 自由能泛函和一个用于有效热轨迹的 Onsager–Machlup 随机作用量中。

Result: The lukewarm branch is shown to correspond exactly to zero entropy production, with a linearized thermal mode whose relaxation time diverges at a critical horizon ratio $ρ_* \approx 0.4354$ as $τ \sim |ρ - ρ_*|^{-1}$. A thermodynamic functional and stochastic action are constructed that reproduce this critical structure.
\n\n
结果表明 lukewarm 分支精确对应于零熵产生，其线性化热模的弛豫时间在临界视界比 $ρ_* \approx 0.4354$ 处以 $τ \sim |ρ - ρ_*|^{-1}$ 发散。作者构建了一个热力学泛函和随机作用量，成功复现了这一临界结构。

Conclusion: The lukewarm Reissner–Nordström–de Sitter solution is not merely a geometric coincidence of equal temperatures but a genuine critical point in the nonequilibrium thermodynamics of a two-horizon system, characterized by vanishing dissipation and divergent relaxation time, and describable through standard statistical-mechanical functionals.
\n\n
lukewarm Reissner–Nordström–de Sitter 解并非仅仅是温度相等的几何巧合，而是双视界系统非平衡热力学中的一个真实临界点，其特征是耗散消失、弛豫时间发散，并可通过标准统计力学泛函加以描述。

Abstract: We reinterpret the lukewarm sector of four-dimensional Reissner--Nordström--de Sitter black holes as the exact zero-dissipation thermal manifold of an effective two-horizon nonequilibrium system. In the fixed-charge sector, the inter-horizon thermal affinity controls the entropy production and vanishes precisely on the lukewarm branch. The corresponding linearized thermal mode is governed by an exact relaxation coefficient \(K_L(ρ)\), with \(ρ=r_+/r_c\), and changes stability at the critical ratio \[ ρ_*=\frac{1+\sqrt{3}-\sqrt{2}\,3^{1/4}}{2}\approx 0.4354, \] where the relaxation time diverges as \(τ\sim |ρ-ρ_*|^{-1}\). We then encode this critical structure in a minimal Bragg--Williams functional and an Onsager--Machlup action for the effective trajectories of the thermal mode. In this way, the lukewarm branch is promoted from a geometric equal-temperature locus to a critical inter-horizon thermal manifold.

</details>

TL;DR: This paper studies how braneworld gravity modifies the spacetime around black holes sourced by bulk matter, showing that finite brane tension and anisotropic Einstein clusters suppress horizon formation and alter optical observables like the black hole shadow and Einstein ring. These effects are most significant for sub-stellar mass black holes and may provide observational constraints on brane tension.
\n\n
本文研究了膜世界引力如何修改由体空间物质源产生的黑洞周围的时空，表明有限膜张力与各向异性爱因斯坦星团共同抑制视界形成，并改变黑洞阴影和爱因斯坦环等光学可观测量。这些效应在亚恒星质量黑洞中最显著，或可用于观测约束膜张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from exploring how higher-dimensional braneworld scenarios—specifically those with localized bulk matter sourcing four-dimensional black holes—affect local gravitational physics and observable signatures. The authors aim to understand whether nonlocal and quadratic curvature corrections inherent in braneworld models can lead to measurable deviations from general relativity, especially in compact astrophysical environments.
\n\n
动机源于探索高维膜世界场景（特别是由局域体空间物质源产生四维黑洞的情形）如何影响局域引力物理和可观测信号。作者旨在理解膜世界模型中固有的非局域项和曲率平方修正是否会导致广义相对论的可观测偏离，尤其是在致密天体环境中。

Method: The authors model the black hole environment using an Einstein cluster—an anisotropic fluid configuration—and incorporate the effective four-dimensional field equations derived from the Randall–Sundrum braneworld scenario, which include both quadratic energy-momentum terms ($\\sim \\lambda^{-1} T_{\\mu\
u}T^{\\mu\
u}$) and nonlocal Weyl contributions from the bulk. They analyze horizon formation conditions and compute the black hole shadow and Einstein ring radii under these modified gravity effects, using current neutron star-based bounds on brane tension $\\lambda$.
\n\n
作者使用爱因斯坦星团（一种各向异性流体构型）对黑洞环境建模，并引入Randall–Sundrum膜世界模型导出的有效四维场方程，其中包含能量-动量张量的二次项（$\\sim \\lambda^{-1} T_{\\mu\
u}T^{\\mu\
u}$）以及来自体空间的非局域Weyl贡献。他们在此修正引力框架下分析视界形成条件，并计算黑洞阴影和爱因斯坦环半径，同时采用基于中子星观测对膜张力$\\lambda$的现有约束。

Result: The combined effect of anisotropy and finite brane tension weakens effective gravity, preventing horizon formation in the surrounding medium. For sub-stellar mass black holes in compact environments, this effect is observationally relevant. The Einstein ring radius decreases as brane tension $\\lambda$ decreases, while the black hole shadow radius increases—despite the overall weakening of gravity—providing potentially complementary probes of $\\lambda$.
\n\n
各向异性与有限膜张力的共同效应削弱了有效引力，阻止了周围介质中视界的形成。对于致密环境中的亚恒星质量黑洞，该效应具有观测意义。随着膜张力$\\lambda$减小，爱因斯坦环半径减小，而黑洞阴影半径反而增大——尽管整体引力被削弱——这为探测$\\lambda$提供了互补的观测手段。

Conclusion: Braneworld corrections significantly alter black hole environments in ways that can be probed through gravitational lensing and horizon-scale imaging. The tension between the shadow and Einstein ring behaviors under varying $\\lambda$ suggests that joint observations could tightly constrain brane tension in specific astrophysical settings, particularly involving low-mass compact objects.
\n\n
膜世界修正以可通过引力透镜和视界尺度成像探测的方式显著改变了黑洞环境。在不同$\\lambda$下黑洞阴影与爱因斯坦环行为之间的张力表明，联合观测可在特定天体物理场景（尤其是涉及低质量致密天体时）对膜张力施加严格限制。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of braneworld theory on the astrophysical environment surrounding a black hole. The black hole is sourced by localized matter from the bulk, which could describe both regular and singular (Schwarzschild) black hole. Employing an Einstein cluster description for the environment, we find that the anisotropic nature of the cluster, coupled with finite brane tension, leads to a weakening of gravity due to the quadratic and nonlocal corrections to the effective four-dimensional field equations. Consequently, this effect prevents horizon formation within the environment. Applying current constraints on the brane tension derived from neutron star observations, we demonstrate that this effect is particularly relevant for sub-stellar mass black holes embedded in compact environments. Furthermore, we investigate the optical signatures of finite brane tension in this scenario, specifically focusing on the black hole shadow and Einstein ring radii. We show that the Einstein ring radius decreases with a smaller brane tension, whereas the black hole shadow radius increases--somewhat contradicts the weakening gravity effects. Ultimately, these two observables may jointly serve to constrain the value of the brane tension in a very specific astrophysical scenarios.

</details>

Abstract: Family puzzle asks why the Standard Model (SM) features exactly 3 families of quarks and leptons. Motivated by topological constraints, we study 4-dimensional fermionic anomalies with discrete $Z_n$ symmetry, classified by the 5d spin bordism group. For minimal nonzero positive integers $N_c$ and $N_f$, we find the minimal color extensions: $N_c=3, N_f \ge 3$; $N_c=4, N_f \ge 2$; and $N_c=12, N_f \ge 6$. If we further require that an SM baryon is a fermion so $N_c$ is odd, then $Z_{N_c}=Z($SU$(N_c))$ color center, we prove 3 families and 3 colors, $N_c=N_f=3$, is the unique case that stands out. We also prove that $A_{Z_3}p_1= 0\mod3$ for the mod 3 cohomology class in an appropriate context.

Abstract: The Swampland Distance Conjecture (SDC) states that, for any infinite-distance limit in the moduli space of a quantum gravity effective field theory (EFT), there should exist an infinite tower of states that become exponentially light. According to the Emergent String Conjecture, such a tower should consist either of tensionless strings or of Kaluza-Klein modes, each with a mass-decay rate that depends in a precise way on the dimension of the effective field theory.
  In this paper, we use the results obtained in arXiv:2508.18401 on the SDC for symmetric moduli spaces and how these rates are encoded in the weight polytope of the corresponding particle-state representations to determine the symmetric space EFTs and representations that have these decay rates. Remarkably, assuming that the particle states transform in an irreducible representation, the list of possible polytopes and moduli spaces is finite. Different EFTs are related by embedding one moduli space in another or by taking a decompactification limit. Requiring compatibility of the particle representations under such branching, we find that, while most of the theories can be obtained from an EFT based on $E_{8(8)}$, there remain three in our list that appear to be impossible to get from M- or string-theory compactifications. Using the same embedding procedure, we also identify the string and brane representations that should be present in the spectrum.

Abstract: We study Abelian $S$-duality of Maxwell theory on $A$-type asymptotically locally Euclidean (ALE) spaces. Unlike on closed four-manifolds, the Maxwell path integral on an ALE space is not naturally a scalar partition function. Rather, it decomposes into theta-function blocks labeled by flat $U(1)$ holonomy sectors on the asymptotic lens-space boundary. We interpret these blocks as components of the Hilbert-space boundary state prepared by the ALE path integral. With this interpretation, the apparent failure of ordinary modularity is replaced by vector-valued modular covariance under the action of the modular group. We test this picture explicitly for Eguchi-Hanson space by gluing it to its orientation reversal. The resulting closed four-manifold is diffeomorphic to $S^2\times S^2$, and the natural pairing of the two ALE boundary states reproduces the standard Maxwell partition function on $S^2\times S^2$. We then refine the construction by turning on electric and magnetic $1$-form symmetry backgrounds. In their presence, the ALE theta blocks are not ordinary functions, but sections of a line bundle over the Cartan torus associated with the $A_{N-1}$ root lattice, reflecting the mixed electric-magnetic $1$-form anomaly. We also discuss gauging discrete $\mathbb Z_k$ subgroups of the $1$-form symmetries and show that the vector-valued boundary-state structure remains the natural covariant framework after gauging. In this sense, ALE spaces behave as chiral building blocks for four-dimensional Maxwell theory: individual ALE blocks carry sector-resolved boundary data, while gluing pairs these sectors to produce an ordinary closed-manifold partition function, much like the pairing of left- and right-moving conformal blocks in two-dimensional CFT.

Abstract: A novel inequality was proposed in \cite{Balasubramanian:2025hxg} for tripartite holographic states, using which it was argued, that purely GHZ-like tripartite entanglement is not allowed in holography. Abstract: We study the finite-temperature Casimir effect for a massless scalar field confined between two parallel plates in a Schwarzschild-like wormhole spacetime. These results provide a compact framework for analyzing quantum vacuum forces in gravitational backgrounds.

This leads them to construct an extended BMS$_3$ algebra by including a weight-one current generator.

作者对具有Carrollian世界面几何的零弦所对应的约束/规范系统进行了细致的Hamiltonian分析。他们将这些系统的对称性结构与有张力弦的Carrollian极限进行对比，识别出一种与Carrollian微分同胚相容的额外局域Weyl型对称性——称为Carroll-Weyl对称性。这促使他们通过引入一个权为1的流生成元来构造扩展的BMS$_3$代数。

Result: The key result is the discovery that null strings possess a Carroll-Weyl gauge symmetry beyond the symmetries obtained via the Carrollian limit of tensile strings. Consequently, the constraint algebra is not merely BMS$_3$, but an extension thereof: $$[\\mathcal{L}_m, \\mathcal{L}_n] = (m-n)\\mathcal{L}_{m+n}, \\quad [\\mathcal{L}_m, \\mathcal{M}_n] = (m-n)\\mathcal{M}_{m+n}, \\quad [\\mathcal{M}_m, \\mathcal{M}_n] = 0,$$ augmented by a weight-one operator $\\mathcal{W}_n$ satisfying $$[\\mathcal{L}_m, \\mathcal{W}_n] = -n \\mathcal{W}_{m+n}, \\quad [\\mathcal{M}_m, \\mathcal{W}_n] = 0, \\quad [\\mathcal{W}_m, \\mathcal{W}_n] = 0.$$ This extended algebra reflects the enhanced gauge invariance of the tensionless theory.

Conclusion: The Carrollian limit of tensile strings does not fully capture the symmetry content of intrinsically tensionless null strings. The intrinsic formulation of null strings requires an extended BMS$_3$ algebra incorporating a Carroll-Weyl symmetry generator of weight one. This underscores the necessity of treating tensionless strings as independent physical systems rather than mere limits of tensile strings.

有张力弦的Carrollian极限并不能完全捕捉内禀无张力零弦的对称性内容。零弦的内禀表述需要一个包含权为1的Carroll-Weyl对称性生成元的扩展BMS$_3$代数。这强调了将无张力弦视为独立物理系统而非仅作为有张力弦极限的必要性。

Abstract: We observed that the null strings, tensionless strings with Carrollian worldsheets, exhibit an extra gauge symmetry, \textit{Carroll-Weyl} gauge symmetry, which cannot be obtained from ultra-relativistic Carrollian limit of tensile strings. Due to the existence of this symmetry, the BMS$_3$ algebra of constraints, which is obtained as the Carrollian limit of two Virasoro algebras of the standard tensile strings, should be replaced with an BMS$_3$ algebra extended by a weight one operator.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from recent observations of violations of the MSS chaos bound in various black hole spacetimes, raising the question of whether such violations are artifacts of modified gravity theories or intrinsic features of black hole geometry. 该研究的动机源于近期在多种黑洞时空中观测到MSS混沌界的破坏，这引发了疑问：此类破坏究竟是修改引力理论的人为产物，还是黑洞几何本身的固有特性？澄清其物理起源——尤其是对极端附近时空结构的依赖性——对于理解量子混沌界在经典引力中的适用范围至关重要。

Method: The authors perform a systematic analysis of circular geodesic stability across black hole solutions in Einstein gravity, scalar-tensor theories, and higher-curvature gravity. They examine the behavior of the Lyapunov exponent $\\lambda_L$ associated with unstable circular orbits in the near-extremal limit, relating it to the surface gravity $\\kappa$ and the proper distance between the photon orbit and the horizon. The key diagnostic is whether the orbit remains outside the horizon as $\\kappa \\to 0$, and how gravitational time dilation affects the instability scale.

作者系统分析了爱因斯坦引力、标量-张量理论和高阶曲率引力中黑洞解的圆轨道稳定性。他们考察了近极端极限下与不稳定圆轨道相关的Lyapunov指数 $\\lambda_L$ 的行为，并将其与表面引力 $\\kappa$ 以及光子轨道与视界之间的固有距离联系起来。关键判据在于当 $\\kappa \\to 0$ 时轨道是否仍位于视界之外，以及引力时间膨胀如何影响不稳定性尺度。

Result: The study finds that violation of the MSS bound ($\\lambda_L > \\kappa$) occurs precisely when the unstable circular orbit (e.g., photon sphere) remains at a finite proper distance from the horizon as surface gravity vanishes. Conversely, when the orbit approaches the degenerate horizon, divergent gravitational redshift suppresses $\\lambda_L$, forcing $\\lambda_L \\to \\kappa$ and saturating the bound. This behavior is universal across diverse gravity theories, indicating a geometric origin independent of UV completion.

研究发现，当表面引力消失时，若不稳定圆轨道（如光子球）与视界保持有限的固有距离，则MSS界（$\\lambda_L > \\kappa$）会被破坏；反之，当轨道趋近视界简并点时，发散的引力红移会压制 $\\lambda_L$，迫使 $\\lambda_L \\to \\kappa$，从而饱和该界。这一行为在多种引力理论中具有普适性，表明其起源于几何结构，与紫外完备性无关。

Conclusion: The paper concludes that the applicability of the MSS chaos bound in classical black hole spacetimes is governed by a purely geometric criterion: the fate of the photon sphere relative to the horizon in the near-extremal limit. This leads to a geometric conjecture that the bound holds if and only if the photon orbit coalesces with the horizon as $\\kappa \\to 0$. 本文得出结论：MSS混沌界在经典黑洞时空中的适用性由一个纯几何判据决定——即近极端极限下光子球相对于视界的命运。由此提出几何猜想：当且仅当 $\\kappa \\to 0$ 时光子轨道与视界合并时，MSS界才成立。该结果揭示了将MSS等量子信息论界推广至经典引力动力学时的根本限制，必须考虑时空几何结构。

Abstract: Violation of the Maldacena-Shenker-Stanford (MSS) chaos bound has been observed in various black hole spacetimes, but its physical origin remains unclear. In particular, it is uncertain whether these violations arise from modifications of general relativity or reflect a more fundamental feature of black hole spacetimes. When the relevant orbit remains outside the horizon as the surface gravity vanishes, the instability scale persists, and the chaos bound can be violated. On the other hand, as the orbit approaches the degenerate horizon, the instability becomes suppressed by the associated divergent gravitational time dilation, ultimately leading to saturation of the bound. Motivated by these results, we propose a geometric conjecture that determines the applicability of the MSS bound directly from the photon-sphere and horizon structure of the spacetime. Our findings identify a universal geometric criterion that governs the applicability of the MSS bound in black hole spacetimes, revealing a fundamental constraint on extending the quantum chaos bound to classical gravitational settings.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce a nonabelian surface holonomy that is constructed from a one-form gauge potential that takes values in a loop algebra of the $U(N)$ gauge group. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to develop a non-stochastic, deterministic formulation of quantum field theory that avoids the conceptual and technical issues of the standard path integral—such as ill-defined measures and reliance on Wick rotation—by reformulating quantization through constrained Hamiltonian dynamics in an auxiliary intrinsic time $τ$. This builds on prior work in quantum mechanics [1] and seeks to establish a rigorous, real-time foundation for QFT amenable to numerical simulation without Euclideanization.

动机是构建一种非随机、确定性的量子场论表述，以规避标准路径积分在测度定义不清和依赖Wick转动等方面的理论与技术困难。该方法通过在辅助内禀时间 $τ$ 中引入约束哈密顿动力学来重构量子化过程，延续了文献[1]中量子力学的工作，旨在建立一个严格的实时间QFT框架，使其适用于无需欧几里得化的数值模拟。

Method: The method involves analytically continuing scalar fields $\\phi(x,t)$ and the action $S$ from real to complex values, introducing an intrinsic time $τ$ that parametrizes a deterministic Hamiltonian flow on phase space. Constraints are imposed to select physically stable trajectories in this extended phase space and to define Lefschetz thimbles (convergent integration cycles) for the microcanonical functional $Z_{\\text{micro}} = \\int \\mathcal{D}\\phi \\, \\delta(H - E) e^{iS}$. The resulting constrained equations of motion are derived from a symplectic structure and solved numerically in $1+1$ dimensions for the free scalar field. Correlation functions and operator identities are then computed directly in real time.
\n\n
方法包括将标量场 $\\phi(x,t)$ 和作用量 $S$ 从实数解析延拓至复数，引入内禀时间 $τ$ 以参数化相空间上的确定性哈密顿流。通过施加约束条件，在扩展相空间中选取物理上稳定的轨迹，并为微正则泛函 $Z_{\\text{micro}} = \\int \\mathcal{D}\\phi \\, \\delta(H - E) e^{iS}$ 定义Lefschetz thimble（即收敛的积分路径）。由此导出的约束运动方程基于辛结构，并在 $1+1$ 维自由标量场情形下进行数值求解。关联函数和算符恒等式均在实时间中直接计算。

This demonstrates consistency with conventional QFT while operating entirely in real time without Wick rotation.

在连续极限下，微正则生成泛函成功复现了标量量子场论的标准Feynman生成泛函。对于 $1+1$ 维自由标量场，数值模拟验证了：(i) 正确的实时间两点Wightman函数；(ii) 正则等时对易关系 $[\\phi(t,x), \\pi(t,y)] = i\\delta(x-y)$；(iii) 满足包含正确接触项的Dyson–Schwinger方程。这表明该方法在完全实时间、无需Wick转动的前提下，与传统QFT保持一致。

Conclusion: Constrained symplectic quantization provides a viable, deterministic, and real-time alternative to the Feynman path integral for scalar quantum field theories. Abstract: Constrained symplectic quantization is a functional formulation of quantum field theory in which quantum fluctuations are sampled through a deterministic Hamiltonian flow in an auxiliary intrinsic time $τ$. In this paper we extend the quantum-mechanical framework introduced in [1] to a relativistic scalar quantum field theory in Minkowski space-time. The construction is based on the analytic continuation of fields and action from $\mathbb{R}$ to $\mathbb{C}$ together with constraints that select stable intrinsic-time trajectories and, at the same time, define convergent integration cycles for the corresponding microcanonical functional. We show that, in the continuum limit, the microcanonical generating functional reproduces the Feynman generating functional. </details>

Abstract: This short communication investigates impurity coupling in generalized field theories where scalar coupling is introduced directly at the level of the kinetic and gradient contributions of the energy. We show that the fundamental aspects of the original theory, which has been previously investigated in the impurity-free setting, can be extended to the inhomogeneous scenario. We show that BPS multi-kink configurations are possible in the model, as well as in the usual half-BPS scalar theories.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from understanding consistent interacting theories of higher-spin fields in extended supersymmetry, particularly $\mathcal{N}=2$ superspace. While higher-spin interactions are highly constrained by consistency (e.g., the Coleman–Mandula theorem), supersymmetry offers a framework to evade some no-go results. The prior work arXiv:2408.00668 introduced $\mathcal{N}=2$ higher-spin supercurrents, but a detailed analysis of the corresponding cubic vertices and their structure was lacking. This paper aims to fill that gap by explicitly constructing the abelian vertices and elucidating the role of analytic supercurrents in encoding interaction data.
\n\n
动机源于理解在扩展超对称（特别是 $\\mathcal{N}=2$ 超空间）中高自旋场的一致相互作用理论。尽管高自旋相互作用受到一致性条件（如Coleman–Mandula定理）的严格限制，但超对称提供了一种规避某些“no-go”结果的框架。先前工作arXiv:2408.00668引入了 $\\mathcal{N}=2$ 高自旋超流，但尚未对相应的三次顶点及其结构进行详细分析。本文旨在通过显式构造阿贝尔顶点并阐明解析超流在编码相互作用信息中的作用来填补这一空白。

Method: The authors employ $\mathcal{N}=2$ harmonic superspace techniques to formulate higher-spin superfields and super-Weyl tensors. They define a principal supercurrent satisfying specific analyticity and differential constraints, from which all conserved supercurrents are derived as descendants. Using this framework, they construct the cubic interaction vertices in superspace by coupling a high-spin superfield to two lower-spin ones, respecting the condition $\mathbf{s_1} \geq 2 \mathbf{s_2}$. The analytic representation of the vertices is obtained via explicit expressions involving the super-Weyl tensors. Component field expansions are performed along the Bel–Robinson diagonal ($\mathbf{s_1} = 2\mathbf{s_2}$). Finally, the superfield inverse Noether procedure is applied to deduce higher-spin gauge transformations for the $\mathcal{N}=2$ vector multiplet in the $(\mathbf{s}, \mathbf{1}, \mathbf{1})$ sector, and their rigid limit is analyzed.
\n\n
作者采用 $\\mathcal{N}=2$ 调和超空间（harmonic superspace）技术来表述高自旋超场和超Weyl张量。他们定义了一个满足特定解析性和微分约束的主超流，并由此导出所有守恒超流作为其后代。在此框架下，他们在超空间中构造三次相互作用顶点，将一个高自旋超场与两个低自旋超场耦合，并满足条件 $\\mathbf{s_1} \\geq 2 \\mathbf{s_2}$。顶点的解析表示通过涉及超Weyl张量的显式表达式获得。沿Bel–Robinson对角线（$\\mathbf{s_1} = 2\\mathbf{s_2}$）进行分量场展开。最后，应用超场逆Noether程序推导 $(\\mathbf{s}, \\mathbf{1}, \\mathbf{1})$ 相互作用中 $\\mathcal{N}=2$ 矢量多重态的高自旋规范变换，并分析其刚性极限。

Result: The key results are: (1) explicit analytic expressions for the $\mathcal{N}=2$ abelian cubic vertices $(\\mathbf{s_1}, \\mathbf{s_2}, \\mathbf{s_2})$; (2) identification of the principal supercurrent and its descendant conserved supercurrents built from $\\mathcal{N}=2$ higher-spin super-Weyl tensors; (3) proof that the vertex is fully determined by three analytic supercurrent components $J^{++}_{\\alpha(s-1)\\dot\\alpha(s-1)}$, $J^+_{\\alpha(s-1)\\dot\\alpha(s-2)}$, and $\\bar{J}^+_{\\alpha(s-2)\\dot\\alpha(s-1)}$; (4) extraction of component interactions on the Bel–Robinson diagonal; and (5) derivation of higher-spin gauge transformations for the $\\mathcal{N}=2$ vector multiplet, which in the rigid limit and for odd $\\mathbf{s}$ reduce to $\\mathcal{N}=2$ supersymmetric generalizations of zilch symmetries.
\n\n
主要结果包括：(1) 给出了 $\\mathcal{N}=2$ 阿贝尔三次顶点 $(\\mathbf{s_1}, \\mathbf{s_2}, \\mathbf{s_2})$ 的显式解析表达式；(2) 确定了主超流及其由 $\\mathcal{N}=2$ 高自旋超Weyl张量构建的后代守恒超流；(3) 证明顶点完全由三个解析超流分量 $J^{++}_{\\alpha(s-1)\\dot\\alpha(s-1)}$、$J^+_{\\alpha(s-1)\\dot\\alpha(s-2)}$ 和 $\\bar{J}^+_{\\alpha(s-2)\\dot\\alpha(s-1)}$ 所决定；(4) 提取了Bel–Robinson对角线上的分量相互作用；(5) 推导出 $\\mathcal{N}=2$ 矢量多重态的高自旋规范变换，在刚性极限下且当 $\\mathbf{s}$ 为奇数时，这些变换退化为zilch对称性的 $\\mathcal{N}=2$ 超对称推广。

Conclusion: The paper establishes a complete and explicit description of a class of $\mathcal{N}=2$ supersymmetric higher-spin cubic interactions using analytic superfields and super-Weyl tensors. It demonstrates that the dynamics of these abelian vertices are entirely encoded in a minimal set of analytic supercurrents, providing a powerful framework for future studies of higher-spin theories in extended superspace. The emergence of zilch-type symmetries in the rigid limit highlights a deep connection between higher-spin conservation laws and hidden symmetries in supersymmetric settings.
\n\n
本文利用解析超场和超Weyl张量，建立了 $\\mathcal{N}=2$ 超对称高自旋三次相互作用某一类别的完整且显式的描述。研究表明，这些阿贝尔顶点的动力学完全编码于一组极小的解析超流中，为未来在扩展超空间中研究高自旋理论提供了有力框架。刚性极限下zilch型对称性的出现，揭示了高自旋守恒律与超对称背景下隐藏对称性之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: In this article we study the structure of the $\mathcal{N}=2$ abelian higher-spin cubic $(\mathbf{s_1}, \mathbf{s_2}, \mathbf{s_2})$ vertices and the corresponding $\mathcal{N}=2$ higher-spin supercurrents, introduced in arXiv:2408.00668. These interactions are possible only for $\mathbf{s_1} \geq 2 \mathbf{s_2}$. Conserved supercurrents are constructed as descendants of the \textit{principal supercurrent}, which is uniquely characterized by simple differential conditions and admits an explicit representation in terms of $\mathcal{N}=2$ higher-spin super-Weyl tensors. We derive the analytic form of the abelian vertices and identify the corresponding analytic higher-spin $\mathcal{N}=2$ supercurrents. We show that the vertex structure is fully determined by the analytic supercurrents $J^{++}_{α(s-1)\dotα(s-1)}$, $J^+_{α(s-1)\dotα(s-2)}$, and $\bar{J}^+_{α(s-2)\dotα(s-1)}$. The analytic form of the vertices provides a simple framework for analyzing their component structure. As an example, we explore the component content of such interactions on the Bel--Robinson diagonal. Using the superfield inverse Noether procedure, we study higher-spin gauge transformations for the $\mathcal{N}=2$ vector multiplet associated with the $(\mathbf{s}, \mathbf{1}, \mathbf{1})$ interaction. In the rigid limit, for odd $\mathbf{s}$ these transformations reduce to the $\mathcal{N}=2$ superspace generalization of zilch-type higher-spin symmetries.

</details>


### [55] [Thermal conformal partial waves from flat-space and defect CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27256)
*K. B. Alkalaev,Semyon Mandrygin,Vladimir Samsonov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper establishes a unified framework connecting conformal partial waves across flat, thermal, and defect geometries via the shadow formalism. It shows that scalar thermal one-point blocks emerge from four-point flat-space and two-point defect blocks through specific operator limits, derives the thermal Casimir equation as a diagonal reduction of the flat-space Casimir system without chemical potentials, and identifies a correspondence between defect blocks with spin-$l$ exchange and thermal one-point blocks for external spin-$l$ operators.
\n\n
本文通过shadow formalism建立了平直、热力学和缺陷背景上conformal partial waves之间的统一对应关系。文章表明，标量热力学单点块可通过特定算符构型从四点平直空间块和两点缺陷块中系统导出；在不引入化学势的情况下，将热力学Casimir方程作为平直空间Casimir系统的对角约化形式推导出来；并进一步证明了具有自旋-$l$交换算符的缺陷两点块对应于外部自旋-$l$算符的热力学单点块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the desire to unify the description of conformal correlation functions across different spacetime backgrounds—specifically flat Euclidean space, thermal (i.e., $S^1 \\times \\mathbb{R}^{d-1}$), and defect CFTs—by leveraging the shadow formalism. Understanding how conformal blocks transform under geometric reductions or analytic continuations can reveal hidden symmetries and simplify computations in thermal or boundary settings.
\n\n
动机源于希望通过shadow formalism统一描述不同时空背景（特别是平直欧几里得空间、热力学背景（即$S^1 \\times \\mathbb{R}^{d-1}$）以及缺陷CFT）下的共形关联函数。理解conformal blocks在几何约化或解析延拓下的变换行为，有助于揭示隐藏对称性，并简化热力学或边界情形中的计算。

Method: The authors employ the shadow formalism to relate conformal partial waves across geometries. They take specific kinematic limits of four-point functions in flat space and two-point functions in defect CFTs to extract thermal one-point blocks. The thermal Casimir equation is derived by imposing diagonal constraints on the flat-space Casimir differential equations, effectively reducing the system without chemical potentials. Spin-$l$ exchanges in defect setups are mapped to external spinning operators in thermal correlators via representation theory.
\n\n
作者采用shadow formalism来关联不同几何背景下的conformal partial waves。他们通过对平直空间四点函数和缺陷CFT中两点函数施加特定运动学极限，提取出热力学单点块。通过在平直空间Casimir微分方程上施加对角约束，有效约化系统（无需引入化学势），从而导出热力学Casimir方程。利用表示论，将缺陷构型中的自旋-$l$交换映射到热力学关联函数中的外部自旋算符。

Result: The key results include: (1) a systematic derivation of scalar thermal one-point conformal blocks from flat-space four-point and defect two-point blocks; (2) a novel derivation of the thermal Casimir equation as a diagonal reduction of the flat-space Casimir system without invoking chemical potentials; and (3) an explicit correspondence between defect two-point blocks with internal spin-$l$ exchange and thermal one-point blocks with external spin-$l$ operators.
\n\n
主要结果包括：(1) 从平直空间四点块和缺陷两点块中系统导出标量热力学单点conformal blocks；(2) 在不引入化学势的前提下，将热力学Casimir方程作为平直空间Casimir系统的对角约化形式给出新推导；(3) 明确建立了内部自旋-$l$交换的缺陷两点块与外部自旋-$l$算符的热力学单点块之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: The work demonstrates that diverse conformal setups—flat, thermal, and defect—are deeply interconnected through the shadow formalism. This geometric unification simplifies the analysis of thermal correlators and provides a powerful algebraic route to derive thermal Casimir equations and spinning blocks, offering new computational tools for finite-temperature CFT and holography.
\n\n
本工作表明，平直、热力学和缺陷等不同共形构型通过shadow formalism深度关联。这种几何统一简化了热力学关联函数的分析，并为推导热力学Casimir方程和自旋块提供了强有力的代数方法，为有限温度CFT及全息对偶提供了新的计算工具。

Abstract: We establish a correspondence between conformal partial waves on flat, thermal, and defect backgrounds using the shadow formalism. We demonstrate that scalar one-point thermal blocks can be systematically obtained from their four-point flat-space and two-point defect counterparts by considering specific operator configurations. This framework allows us to derive the thermal Casimir equation as a diagonal reduction of the flat-space Casimir system without introducing chemical potentials. We further show that defect two-point blocks with a spin-$l$ exchange operator correspond to thermal one-point blocks for an external spin-$l$ operator.

</details>


### [56] [Wilson coefficients from a non-renormalization theorem in 2D SYM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27359)
*Kabir Bajaj*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper computes the Wilson coefficient of the leading irrelevant deformation—the Dijkgraaf-Verlinde-Verlinde (DVV) operator—in the IR symmetric product orbifold CFT $(\\mathbb{R}^8)^N/S_N$ that describes matrix string theory, using non-renormalization theorems from the UV 2D $\\mathcal{N}=(8,8)$ $U(N)$ Yang-Mills theory. The result confirms the conjectured relation between the Yang-Mills coupling $g_{\\rm YM}$ and the type-IIA string coupling $g_s$, providing a first-principles verification of matrix string theory duality.
\n\n
本文利用紫外（UV）二维 $\\mathcal{N}=(8,8)$ $U(N)$ Yang-Mills 理论中的非重整化定理，计算了矩阵弦理论中红外（IR）对称积轨形共形场论 $(\\mathbb{R}^8)^N/S_N$ 的主导无关形变——即 Dijkgraaf-Verlinde-Verlinde（DVV）算符的 Wilson 系数。该结果验证了 Yang-Mills 耦合常数 $g_{\\rm YM}$ 与 IIA 型弦耦合 $g_s$ 之间的猜想关系，为矩阵弦理论对偶性提供了第一性原理的检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Matrix string theory posits a duality between 2D maximally supersymmetric $U(N)$ Yang-Mills theory and type-IIA string theory. While the IR fixed point of the gauge theory is known to be the symmetric product orbifold CFT $(\\mathbb{R}^8)^N/S_N$, the leading correction away from this fixed point is governed by the DVV twist operator, whose Wilson coefficient encodes the precise map between gauge and string couplings. This coefficient was previously undetermined from first principles, motivating its computation via UV non-renormalization properties.
\n\n
矩阵弦理论提出二维最大超对称 $U(N)$ Yang-Mills 理论与 IIA 型弦理论之间的对偶性。虽然该规范理论的红外不动点已知为对称积轨形共形场论 $(\\mathbb{R}^8)^N/S_N$，但偏离该不动点的主导修正由 DVV 扭曲算符控制，其 Wilson 系数编码了规范耦合与弦耦合之间的精确映射。此前该系数缺乏第一性原理的确定，因此有必要通过紫外非重整化性质进行计算。

Method: The authors exploit non-renormalization theorems in 2D $\\mathcal{N}=(8,8)$ supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory, which protect certain BPS operators and their correlation functions from quantum corrections. By matching protected quantities between the UV gauge theory and the IR orbifold CFT deformed by the DVV operator, they fix the Wilson coefficient of the leading irrelevant deformation without relying on indirect string-theoretic arguments.
\n\n
作者利用二维 $\\mathcal{N}=(8,8)$ 超对称 Yang-Mills 理论中的非重整化定理，该定理保护某些 BPS 算符及其关联函数免受量子修正。通过将紫外规范理论中受保护的物理量与由 DVV 算符形变的红外轨形共形场论进行匹配，他们在不依赖间接弦论论证的情况下确定了主导无关形变的 Wilson 系数。

Result: The Wilson coefficient of the Dijkgraaf-Verlinde-Verlinde operator is determined exactly, yielding the relation $g_{\\rm YM}^2 = g_s$, consistent with the matrix string theory conjecture. This provides a direct, first-principles derivation of the gauge-string coupling dictionary in this duality.
\n\n
Dijkgraaf-Verlinde-Verlinde 算符的 Wilson 系数被精确确定，得出关系式 $g_{\\rm YM}^2 = g_s$，与矩阵弦理论的猜想一致。这为该对偶性中规范-弦耦合字典提供了直接的第一性原理推导。

Conclusion: The successful determination of the leading Wilson coefficient using UV non-renormalization confirms a key prediction of matrix string theory and demonstrates that protected data in the gauge theory can fix deformations of the dual string background. The method opens the door to computing higher-order irrelevant couplings in the IR CFT using similar supersymmetric protection mechanisms.
\n\n
利用紫外非重整化成功确定主导 Wilson 系数，验证了矩阵弦理论的关键预言，并表明规范理论中的受保护数据可以固定对偶弦背景的形变。该方法为利用类似的超对称保护机制计算红外共形场论中更高阶无关耦合开辟了道路。

Abstract: Matrix string theory (arXiv:hep-th/9703030, arXiv:hep-th/9701025, arXiv:hep-th/9710009) is a conjectured duality between two-dimensional maximally supersymmetric $U(N)$ Yang-Mills theory and type-IIA string theory in ten-dimensional Minkowski spacetime. The IR description of this gauge theory is governed by the symmetric product orbifold $(\mathbb{R}^8)^N/S_N$ CFT. The leading irrelevant deformation from this IR fixed point is the Dijkgraaf-Verlinde-Verlinde operator, which comes with an unknown Wilson coefficient. We determine this coefficient using non-renormalization arguments from the UV gauge theory. The result is consistent with the matrix string theory conjecture and gives a first-principles check of the relation between $g_{\rm YM}$ and the string coupling. We also comment on the prospects for fixing further Wilson coefficients using similar methods.

</details>

### [57] [Probing Axion Nucleon Coupling with Optomechanical Frequency Shift Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26170)
*Jiawei Li,Ka-di Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: We propose a laboratory-scale axion detection method using a levitated optomechanical sensor to probe spin-independent forces from two-axion exchange between a microsphere and metal substrates, achieving up to 100× better constraints on axion-nucleon couplings for axion masses $m_a \\in [0.1, 1]\\,\\text{eV}$.
\n\n
我们提出一种基于悬浮光力学传感器的实验室尺度轴子探测方案，通过测量微球与金属基底间由双轴子交换产生的自旋无关力，在轴子质量 $m_a \\in [0.1, 1]\\,\\text{eV}$ 范围内将轴子-核子耦合的上限提升达两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current experimental approaches to detect axionic dark matter—such as spin-precession experiments and tests of the inverse-square law—are limited in sensitivity or mass range. A complementary method is needed to probe spin-independent axion-mediated forces at short ranges with enhanced precision, particularly for eV-scale axions where astrophysical bounds weaken.
\n\n
当前探测轴子暗物质的实验方法（如自旋进动实验和平方反比律检验）在灵敏度或质量范围上存在局限。需要一种互补方法，以更高精度探测短程下由轴子介导的自旋无关力，特别是在天体物理限制变弱的 eV 量级轴子区域。

Method: The experiment employs a dual-channel optomechanical setup with a levitated dielectric microsphere positioned near alternating aluminum and silver mirrors. The differential optical readout measures the force gradient induced by two-axion exchange between nucleons in the sphere and the substrate. This interaction shifts the cavity resonance frequencies, producing a measurable splitting in optical transmission peaks proportional to the axion-mediated potential $V(r) \\propto g_{aNN}^2 e^{-m_a r}/r^3$. By comparing responses across two mirror materials with different nuclear compositions, systematic effects are suppressed while isolating the axion signal.
\n\n
该实验采用双通道光力学装置，将介电微球悬浮于交替切换的铝和银镜面附近。差分光学读出测量微球与基底中核子间双轴子交换所产生的力梯度。该相互作用改变腔共振频率，在光学透射峰中产生可测的分裂，其大小正比于轴子介导势 $V(r) \\propto g_{aNN}^2 e^{-m_a r}/r^3$。通过比较两种不同核组成的镜面响应，抑制系统误差并提取轴子信号。

Result: For symmetric axion-nucleon coupling ($g_{a p} = g_{a n} = g_{aNN}$), the proposed dual-cavity platform sets new upper limits on $g_{aNN}$ that improve upon existing laboratory and astrophysical constraints by up to two orders of magnitude in the axion mass window $m_a \\in [0.1, 1]\\,\\text{eV}$.
\n\n
对于对称轴子-核子耦合（$g_{a p} = g_{a n} = g_{aNN}$），所提出的双腔平台在轴子质量窗口 $m_a \\in [0.1, 1]\\,\\text{eV}$ 内设定了新的 $g_{aNN}$ 上限，相比现有实验室和天体物理约束提升达两个数量级。

Conclusion: Levitated optomechanics provides a powerful, table-top platform for probing ultralight bosonic dark matter through spin-independent forces. The dual-substrate differential scheme effectively isolates exotic interactions from backgrounds, enabling competitive constraints on axion-nucleon couplings in a theoretically motivated mass range previously inaccessible to short-range fifth-force experiments.
\n\n
悬浮光力学为通过自旋无关力探测超轻玻色子暗物质提供了一种强大的桌面级平台。双基底差分方案能有效从背景中分离出新相互作用，从而在短程第五力实验此前难以触及但理论动机明确的轴子质量区间内，获得具有竞争力的轴子-核子耦合限制。

Abstract: The search for non-baryonic dark matter remains a key focus in modern physics, with the light pseudoscalar axion serving as a well-motivated candidate. Here, we present a laboratory-scale detection scheme to constrain axion-nucleon interactions using a levitated optomechanical sensor, complementing conventional spin-precession and inverse-square-law tests. By monitoring a micro-spherical test mass levitated near alternative aluminum and silver substrate mirrors, our dual-channel differential readout extracts the spin-independent force gradient generated by two-axion exchange. This approach translates the short-range interaction directly into a resolvable splitting in the optical transmission peaks. Our evaluation indicates that for symmetric nucleon coupling , the dual-cavity platform establishes competitive upper bounds, improving upon existing constraints by up to two orders of magnitude within the $m_{a}$ in [0.1, 1]eV mass range.

</details>


### [58] [Electromagnetic Signatures From Primordial Black Holes in the Solar System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26204)
*Alexandra P. Klipfel,David I. Kaiser*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper investigates the detectability of Hawking radiation from asteroid-mass primordial black holes (PBHs) that could constitute all dark matter, focusing on local transits through the inner Solar System and explosions in the outer Solar System. It evaluates current and proposed instruments across the electromagnetic spectrum and finds that missions like AMEGO-X could detect nearby PBH transits, while HAWC and LHAASO may observe PBH explosions within ~0.1–0.5 pc. The authors also assess whether a recent ultrahigh-energy neutrino event (KM3-230213A) could be linked to a PBH explosion, concluding it would not have produced a detectable electromagnetic signal, but future similar events might.
\n\n
本文研究了可能构成全部暗物质的小行星质量原初黑洞（Primordial Black Holes, PBHs）所发出的霍金辐射的可探测性，重点关注其穿越内太阳系的近距离飞越事件以及在外太阳系边缘的爆炸事件。文章评估了覆盖从射电到超高能γ射线波段的现有及拟议观测设备，发现如AMEGO-X等任务可探测距地球约0.1 AU内的PBH飞越事件，而HAWC和LHAASO分别可在约0.1 pc和0.5 pc距离内探测PBH爆炸。作者还探讨了近期KM3NeT合作组观测到的超高能中微子事件KM3-230213A是否可能源于PBH爆炸，结论是该事件因天区位置、假设距离及HAWC当时离线状态而无法产生可探测的电磁信号，但未来类似距离的PBH爆炸若与探测器视场对齐，则可能被观测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from renewed interest in asteroid-mass primordial black holes ($10^{17}\,{\rm g} \lesssim M \lesssim 10^{23}\,{\rm g}$) as a compelling dark matter candidate. Since such PBHs emit Hawking radiation with peak photon frequencies spanning infrared to γ-ray bands, they offer potential electromagnetic signatures. The paper aims to quantify the observational prospects for detecting these signals—either from PBHs transiting near Earth or from their final explosive evaporation—using existing and upcoming instruments, and to explore possible connections to recent high-energy astrophysical anomalies like the KM3-230213A neutrino event.

Abstract: Primordial black holes (PBHs) in the asteroid-mass range, with typical masses $10^{17}\,{\rm g}\lesssim M \lesssim 10^{23}\,{\rm g}$, have drawn significant recent attention as a viable dark matter candidate. The peak frequencies of photons emitted via Hawking radiation from asteroid-mass PBHs range from infrared to $γ$-ray bands. We evaluate prospects for detecting Hawking-radiated photons from local PBH transits through the inner Solar System and from PBH explosions in the far outer edges of the Solar System. Abstract: This paper is organized around three main objectives. First, I review in a pedagogical way the unitarity bounds for form factors in $B$-meson decays, together with the parametrizations most commonly used in phenomenological analyses. These include BGL, BCL, CLN, and the Dispersive Matrix (DM) method. Method: The paper employs a kinetic model of the four-step \mucf cycle: (1) muonic-atom formation, (2) muon transfer to tritium, (3) resonant formation of the $(dt\\mu)$ molecular ion, and (4) D--T fusion with possible muon release or alpha-sticking. Rate equations are used to compute the average number of fusions per muon ($X_{\\mu}$). The authors then evaluate enhancement strategies—nuclear-spin and muon dual polarization, in-flight fusion, electric-field-assisted muon recovery, and high-density operation—and combine them into a four-dimensional synergistic scheme. Abstract: Muon-catalyzed nuclear fusion (\mucf) replaces atomic electrons with negative muons, compressing atomic orbitals by about two orders of magnitude and enabling deuterium--tritium (D--T) fusion under near-room-temperature conditions. This paper reviews the physical principles of \mucf{} and formulates its essential dynamics as a four-step cycle: muonic-atom formation, muon transfer, resonant \dtmu{} molecular formation, and D--T fusion with muon release and recycling. A kinetic model is used to quantify the number of catalysis cycles per muon and the corresponding energy gain. We focus on the central limitation of catalytic efficiency, namely the alpha-sticking effect, and discuss possible breakthrough routes including nuclear-spin and muon dual polarization, in-flight muon-catalyzed fusion, and heavy-ion-driven magneto-inertial fusion. Within the idealized assumptions of the present model, a four-dimensional synergistic scheme combining dual polarization, high-density confinement, electric-field-assisted muon recovery, and resonant enhancement may increase the number of catalysis cycles per muon from the present experimental record of about 150 to more than 500, potentially enabling an energy gain \(Q>2\). On this basis, we propose a conceptual fusion--fission fuel-breeding hybrid reactor, denoted as \mucf-FBR, which exploits the 14.1-MeV neutron yield of \mucf{} to breed \({}^{239}\mathrm{Pu}\) from a \({}^{238}\mathrm{U}\) blanket in a decoupled fusion--fission operating mode. This concept may offer advantages in engineering robustness, radiation-damage tolerance, and natural-uranium utilization.

### [61] [Contributions of interference and non-interference components to CP asymmetries in heavy meson decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26480)
*Jing-Juan Qi,Yi-Fan Zhao,Jin-Xia Liu,Zhen-Hua Zhang,Xin-Heng Guo,Zhen-Yang Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper proposes a novel phase-space partitioning method using zeros of Legendre polynomials and sign-function weighting to define new CP asymmetry observables $\mathcal{A}_{\pm}^{\mathrm{asy},l}$ and $\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{CP}}^{\mathrm{asy},l}$ that can isolate interference effects among resonances in multi-body heavy meson decays, which conventional integrated CP asymmetries miss. Applied to $B^\pm\rightarrowπ^\pmπ^+π^-$ near the $ρ^0(1450)$ resonance using LHCb data, odd-$l$ schemes capture interference contributions while even-$l$ schemes highlight non-interference terms.
\n\n
本文提出了一种基于勒让德多项式零点和符号函数加权的新相空间划分方法，定义了新的CP不对称观测量 $\mathcal{A}_{\pm}^{\mathrm{asy},l}$ 和 $\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{CP}}^{\mathrm{asy},l}$，以分离多体重介子衰变中共振之间的干涉效应，而传统积分CP不对称量无法捕捉这些信息。该方法应用于LHCb数据中 $B^\pm\rightarrowπ^\pmπ^+π^-$ 衰变道在 $ρ^0(1450)$ 共振附近的区域，结果表明奇数-$l$ 方案能有效提取干涉项，而偶数-$l$ 方案对非干涉项更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conventional CP asymmetry observables in multi-body heavy meson decays integrate over the full phase space, thereby averaging out higher-order angular momentum (partial wave) contributions and losing sensitivity to interference between different resonant amplitudes. This obscures crucial dynamical information encoded in the decay amplitude's phase structure. The motivation is to construct observables that retain this interference information by exploiting angular correlations through a systematic phase-space decomposition.
\n\n
在多体重介子衰变中，传统的CP不对称观测量通过对整个相空间积分，平均掉了高阶角动量（分波）贡献，从而丧失了对不同共振振幅之间干涉效应的敏感性，掩盖了衰变振幅相位结构中包含的关键动力学信息。本文的动机是通过系统性的相空间分解，利用角关联构造能够保留此类干涉信息的新观测量。

Method: The authors introduce a phase-space partitioning scheme based on the zeros of Legendre polynomials $P_l(\cos\\theta)$, where $\\theta$ is an appropriate helicity or scattering angle in the decay. Each bin is assigned a weight of $\\pm1$ according to the sign of $P_l(\\cos\\theta)$, yielding sign-weighted integrals over phase space. This defines two key observables: an asymmetry $\mathcal{A}_{\pm}^{\mathrm{asy},l}$ and its CP-conjugate difference $\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{CP}}^{\mathrm{asy},l}$. The total amplitude squared is decomposed into interference and non-interference (diagonal) parts, and the response of each observable to these components is analyzed as a function of $l$.
\n\n
作者提出一种基于勒让德多项式 $P_l(\cos\\theta)$ 零点的相空间划分方案，其中 $\\theta$ 是衰变中的适当螺旋度角或散射角。每个区间根据 $P_l(\\cos\\theta)$ 的符号赋予 $\\pm1$ 权重，从而构造带符号加权的相空间积分。由此定义两个关键观测量：不对称量 $\mathcal{A}_{\pm}^{\mathrm{asy},l}$ 及其CP共轭差 $\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{CP}}^{\mathrm{asy},l}$。将总振幅平方分解为干涉项与非干涉项（对角项），并分析各观测量对这些成分的响应如何依赖于 $l$。

Result: When applied to the $B^\pm\\rightarrow\\pi^\pm\\pi^+\\pi^-$ decay near the $\\rho^0(1450)$ resonance using LHCb data, the analysis shows that observables with odd $l$ are dominated by interference contributions between different partial waves (e.g., S–D or P–F wave interference), whereas even $l$ observables primarily reflect non-interference (incoherent sum of squared amplitudes) terms. This demonstrates the scheme’s ability to disentangle coherent and incoherent dynamics in multi-body decays.
\n\n
将该方法应用于LHCb数据中 $B^\pm\\rightarrow\\pi^\pm\\pi^+\\pi^-$ 衰变在 $\\rho^0(1450)$ 共振附近的区域，结果显示奇数 $l$ 的观测量主要由不同分波之间的干涉贡献主导（例如S–D或P–F波干涉），而偶数 $l$ 的观测量主要反映非干涉项（振幅平方的非相干叠加）。这证明了该方案能够有效分离多体衰变中的相干与非相干动力学。

Conclusion: The proposed Legendre-polynomial-based phase-space partitioning with sign weighting provides a powerful and systematic framework to extract interference-sensitive CP-violating observables in multi-body decays. The clear separation between odd-$l$ (interference-dominated) and even-$l$ (non-interference-dominated) observables offers a new tool for amplitude analyses and may be generalized to other three- or higher-body decay channels, enhancing the precision of tests of the Standard Model and searches for new physics.
\n\n
所提出的基于勒让德多项式零点并结合符号加权的相空间划分方法，为在多体衰变中提取对干涉敏感的CP破坏观测量提供了一个强大且系统的框架。奇数-$l$（干涉主导）与偶数-$l$（非干涉主导）观测量之间的清晰分离，为振幅分析提供了新工具，并可推广至其他三体或更高体衰变道，从而提升对标准模型检验及新物理搜寻的精度。

Abstract: In multi-body decays of heavy mesons, conventional CP asymmetry observables obtained by integrating over the full phase space are insensitive to the higher-order wave expansion contributions in the decay amplitude squared, and consequently fail to retain information on interference effects among different resonances. We further separate the observables into interference and non-interference parts and analyze their respective roles. As an application, the decay channel $B^\pm\rightarrowπ^\pmπ^+π^-$ are analyzed in the region near the $ρ^0(1450)$ resonance. Using the LHCb data, the results show that odd-$l$
  schemes are particularly effective in isolating interference contributions, while even-$l$ schemes are more sensitive to non-interference terms. This new assignment scheme has the potential to be extended to other decay processes, thus enriching the available physical observables.

Special attention is given to two- and three-channel cases and the Flatté parametrization. They then apply this formalism to the $ΛN$-$ΣN$ coupled-channel system with total charge $Q=+1$, using both simplified toy models and realistic interactions from N$^2$LO chiral effective field theory (χEFT) to compute quantitative cross sections.

Conclusion: Isospin-breaking effects, though small, play a crucial role in shaping threshold cusp structures in multichannel hadronic scattering. Abstract: We discuss the isospin-breaking effects on threshold cusp structures in multichannel scattering near two-body thresholds. In hadronic systems with isospin symmetry, two or more nearly degenerate thresholds can appear, and their small splitting due to isospin breaking can generate multiple cusp structures in a narrow energy region. Finally, we discuss the cusp structures in the $Λp$ elastic cross section in the coupled $ΛN$-$ΣN$ system with charge $Q=+1$. We show that, when isospin breaking is small, the two cusp structures are constrained by isospin symmetry. However, for the specific hadronic decay channel $\\tau^\\pm \\to \\pi^\\pm \\nu_\\tau$, the system is constrained enough to permit only two discrete kinematic solutions. Method: The authors formulate the kinematic constraints from energy-momentum conservation and the on-shell conditions for the $\\tau$ leptons and pions. They reinterpret this system geometrically as the intersection of conic sections in momentum space, revealing the intrinsic two-fold ambiguity. To solve it numerically, they employ singular value decomposition (SVD) to handle the rank-deficient linearized system derived from the constraints, enabling robust extraction of both physical solutions without assuming intermediate resonances. The algorithm uses only measured four-momenta of the pions and beam energy.
\n\n
作者从能量-动量守恒以及 $\\tau$ 轻子和π介子的在壳（on-shell）条件出发建立运动学约束，导出关于未知中微子动量的二次方程组。他们将此方程组在动量空间中几何地诠释为圆锥曲线的交点，揭示了内在的二重模糊性。为数值求解，他们采用奇异值分解（Singular Value Decomposition, SVD）处理由约束导出的秩亏线性化系统，从而稳健地提取两个物理解，且无需假设中间共振态的存在。该算法仅使用π介子的测量四动量和束流能量。

Conclusion: This work provides a practical, resonance-agnostic method for $\\tau$-pair kinematic reconstruction in the $\\pi\\pi$ decay channel, grounded in geometric insight and numerical linear algebra. Abstract: The neutrinos produced in $τ$ decays cannot be directly detected, making the reconstruction of $τ$ kinematics challenging and affecting measurements of quantum correlations such as spin entanglement. Conclusion: The paper establishes a unified description of attractor dynamics for spin density across early and late times in relativistic spin hydrodynamics, demonstrating that distinct classes of second-order corrections play qualitatively different roles: source terms perturb around fixed points, while self-coupling terms reshape the fixed point structure itself. Abstract: We investigate the attractor of spin density in relativistic spin hydrodynamics using Zubarev's non-equilibrium statistical operator formalism in the spin probe limit. We derive the (0+1)D Bjorken flow equations and the associated attractor equation while retaining second order gradient corrections in the relevant relaxation constitutive equations including couplings associated with nonlinear response and nonlocal memory effects. Abstract: The Lund plane offers a physics-motivated, hierarchical representation of QCD radiation within jets, while transformer-based taggers have reached state-of-the-art performance by learning directly from raw particle constituents and their pairwise relations. We investigate whether transformers implicitly capture hierarchical QCD structure from constituent-level inputs, or whether explicit physics representations remain complementary. To test this, we introduce PLuM, a multimodal architecture that projects particle constituents and Lund plane splittings into a shared latent space, processing both jointly with a unified transformer. Cross-attention allows the model to probe whether structured QCD information provides discriminating power beyond what particles alone encode. We observe systematic gains for top-quark and $\mathrm{H}\to\mathrm{b}\bar{\mathrm{b}}$ tagging, while finding no comparable improvement for $\mathrm{H}\to\mathrm{c}\bar{\mathrm{c}}$ or $\mathrm{H}\to 4\mathrm{q}$ topologies. This selective enhancement suggests that explicit hierarchical information about b-jet formation remains complementary to raw particle representations even in highly expressive architectures, while other topologies are already well-captured at constituent level. For high-impact LHC analyses such as Lorentz-boosted di-Higgs searches in the four $\mathrm{b}$ quark final state ($\mathrm{H}\mathrm{H}(4\mathrm{b})$), the gains are substantial: at a $25\%$ di-Higgs efficiency working point, PLuM achieves $25\%$ higher background rejection than the baseline. Our results indicate that physically structured representations of QCD radiation retain discriminating value in the transformer era, motivating further study into how different aspects of jet dynamics are encoded by deep learning algorithms.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: The analysis computes BBN yields in the presence of energy injection from decaying relics $φ \to XX$, where $X$ are SM particles. Key methodological improvements include: (1) using updated primordial $^4$He and D/H abundance measurements and latest nuclear reaction rates; (2) generating electromagnetic and hadronic shower spectra via PYTHIA 8 to accurately model final-state radiation and hadronisation; (3) implementing a refined treatment of $p\leftrightarrow n$ interconversions that includes dynamical weak equilibrium, kaon-induced reactions ($K^\pm + n/p \to \pi + p/n$), and updated cross sections; (4) employing an extended hadrodisintegration formalism that accounts for nuclear destruction during active BBN (not just post-BBN); and (5) self-consistently evaluating the freeze-in yield from inverse decays $XX \to φ$.
\n\n
该分析计算了在衰变遗迹粒子 $φ \to XX$（其中 $X$ 为标准模型粒子）注入能量情况下的 BBN 核素产额。关键方法改进包括：(1) 采用更新的原初 $^4$He 和 D/H 丰度测量值及最新核反应率；(2) 利用 PYTHIA 8 生成电磁与强子簇射谱，以精确模拟末态辐射和强子化；(3) 引入更完善的 $p\leftrightarrow n$ 相互转换处理，包含动态弱平衡、介子诱导反应（如 $K^\pm + n/p \to \pi + p/n$）及更新的截面；(4) 采用扩展的强子致解离形式体系，考虑 BBN 活跃期间（而非仅 BBN 结束后）的核素破坏过程；(5) 自洽地评估来自逆衰变 $XX \to φ$ 的 freeze-in 产额。

Result: The study produces updated exclusion contours in the $(m_φ, τ_φ, Y_φ)$ parameter space for each two-body decay channel (e.g., $φ \to q\bar{q}, gg, e^+e^-, \mu^+\mu^-, \tau^+\tau^-, \nu\bar{\nu}, \gamma\gamma, Z\gamma$, etc.). Constraints are significantly strengthened compared to earlier works, particularly for hadronic channels due to improved modeling of interconversions and in-situ hadrodisintegration. Conclusion: By integrating modern observational data, advanced particle shower simulations, and refined nuclear/weak interaction physics, this work provides the most comprehensive and robust BBN constraints to date on long-lived BSM scalars decaying to SM particles. These results are crucial for testing scenarios such as gravitino, moduli, or axion-like particle decays in supersymmetric or string-motivated cosmologies, and highlight the necessity of consistent treatment of both pre- and post-BBN energy injection effects.

Abstract: We present updated and improved Big Bang Nucleosynthesis (BBN) constraints on heavy, long-lived beyond the Standard Model (BSM) relics $φ$ decaying into pairs of Standard Model particles, covering a comprehensive set of two-body decay channels. We treat the leading effects of these injections in detail, discussing the modification of the neutron-to-proton ratio from hadronic interconversions, as well as hadro- and photodisintegration of the light elements. Our analysis incorporates several important refinements with respect to earlier work. We adopt up-to-date primordial abundance measurements, including the new $^4$He determination and the latest nuclear reaction rates. The hadronic and electromagnetic injection spectra are computed using PYTHIA 8, providing a proper treatment of final-state radiation and hadronisation. We further implement an improved treatment of $p\leftrightarrow n$ interconversions, accounting for dynamical equilibrium, kaon-induced processes, and updated rates. Additionally, we make use of a refined hadrodisintegration formalism which allows us to also consider disintegration processes while BBN is still active. Together, these improvements yield updated exclusion contours on lifetime, mass, and abundance of the relic for each decay channel considered. Furthermore, we discuss the irreducible freeze-in contribution from inverse decays.

Abstract: Recent years have brought a dense sequence of experimental discoveries in heavy-flavor hadron spectroscopy. Heavy-flavor spectroscopy has entered a period in which new hadronic structures are no longer isolated surprises but recurring features across several flavor sectors, seen by multiple experiments in several decay environments. For systems with charm and bottom quarks, smaller widths and cleaner signatures expose regularities that would be harder to isolate in the light-quark sector. This contribution focuses on the classes of states that now define the modern ``exotic'' landscape: hidden-charm pentaquarks, charged charmonium-like structures, resonances in onia-onia systems, doubly-heavy tetraquarks, and open-flavor tetraquarks.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the apparent disconnect between perturbative and nonperturbative aspects of Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD): while high-energy collider physics focuses on parton evolution within protons, high-energy astrophysics interprets gamma-ray spectra often through leptonic models. The paper seeks to unify these domains by proposing that nonperturbative gluon condensation—a phenomenon arising from nonlinear QCD dynamics—can simultaneously explain anomalous spectral features in cosmic gamma rays and reveal novel proton substructure, thereby bridging nuclear/particle physics with astrophysics.

Method: The method centers on the Zhu-Shen-Ruan (ZSR) equation, a nonlinear integro-differential evolution equation derived from structural symmetry principles in QCD. They investigate chaotic solutions and the interplay between shadowing (gluon recombination at low-$x$) and antishadowing (enhancement at moderate-$x$), which together induce a critical accumulation of gluons at a specific momentum scale $Q_c$. This leads to the formation of a gluon-condensed phase. The resulting modification to the parton distribution functions (PDFs) is then mapped to gamma-ray production via hadronic interactions (e.g., $pp \\to \\pi^0 \\to \\gamma\\gamma$), predicting a broken-power-law in the observed spectrum.

方法核心是朱-沈-阮（ZSR）方程——一个基于QCD结构对称性原理导出的非线性积分-微分演化方程。作者分析其与已知线性演化方程（大-$x$ 区的DGLAP、小-$x$ 区的BFKL）及其非线性推广（GLR-MQ）的自洽性。他们研究该方程的混沌解以及遮蔽效应（低-$x$ 胶子复合）与反遮蔽效应（中等-$x$ 增强）之间的协同作用，二者共同诱导胶子在特定动量标度 $Q_c$ 附近发生临界聚集，形成胶子凝聚相。由此导致的部分子分布函数（PDFs）修正被映射到强子相互作用产生的伽马射线过程（如 $pp \\to \\pi^0 \\to \\gamma\\gamma$），预言观测谱中出现断幂律特征。

Conclusion: The paper concludes that gluon condensation is not merely a theoretical curiosity in extreme QCD but a potentially observable phenomenon linking particle physics and astrophysics. \n\n
本文结论认为，胶子凝聚不仅是极端条件下QCD的理论奇想，更可能是连接粒子物理与天体物理的可观测现象。若伽马射线数据中预测的谱特征得到证实，将开辟探测质子结构与非微扰QCD的新窗口，并为核物质中的π介子凝聚乃至凝聚态物理中的类比现象提供跨学科研究入口。未来高精度多信使观测与对撞实验将对此范式进行关键检验。

Abstract: Quark-gluon dynamics within protons and high-energy radiation phenomena in the universe are typically regarded as two entirely distinct fields. This paper aims to demonstrate that gluon condensation (GC) may serve as a direct bridge between these two fields. We review three key aspects of GC research: first, the Zhu-Shen-Ruan (ZSR) equation, as a nonlinear evolution equation based on structural symmetry, exhibits self-consistent connections with the DGLAP, BFKL and GLR-MQ-ZRS equations, providing a theoretical foundation for the generation of GC; second, the chaotic solutions and the shadowing-antishadowing synergy inherent in this equation can drive gluons to aggregate near the critical momentum, thereby forming a novel type of high-density, strongly interacting matter; third, these changes in microstructure manifest themselves as a broken-power-law feature in high-energy cosmic gamma-ray spectra, thereby offering new insights into the hadronic scenarios underlying certain astrophysical sources. Consequently, GC not only concerns the novel behaviour of quantum chromodynamics under extreme conditions but may also serve as a vital window for probing the deep structure of protons using cosmic-ray signals. With the advancement of higher-precision gamma-ray observations, hadron collision experiments and related theoretical research, the physical picture of GC and its observational criteria are expected to undergo more rigorous testing. Should this picture be confirmed, certain features in the high-energy gamma-ray spectrum will need to be re-examined within the deeper context of hadronic dynamics; simultaneously, GC may also provide a new entry point for research into pion condensation in nuclear physics and even condensed matter physics. Consequently, the significance of the search for GC extends beyond the model itself, reaching into multiple fields of natural science.

</details>

*Zhi-zhong Xing*

TL;DR: The paper identifies a novel family-separated solution to the canonical seesaw equation, yielding simple relations between seesaw parameters and observable quantities, and predicts a direct correlation between CP violation in light neutrino oscillations and heavy Majorana neutrino decays.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The canonical seesaw mechanism, while theoretically compelling, typically involves high-dimensional parameter spaces that obscure direct connections between high-scale physics and low-energy observables. The authors aim to uncover a special class of solutions to the exact seesaw formula that simplifies this relationship on a per-family basis, thereby enhancing testability.
\n\n
标准seesaw机制虽然理论上有吸引力，但通常涉及高维参数空间，模糊了高能物理与低能可观测量之间的直接联系。作者旨在发现精确seesaw公式的特殊解类，使其在每一代中微子上简化这种关系，从而提高可检验性。

Method: The authors solve the exact seesaw relation $m_i = - (M_i R_{\\alpha i}^2)/U_{\\alpha i}^2$ under the assumption of family-wise separation, where each light-heavy neutrino pair $(i)$ is treated independently. This yields the condition $m_i/M_i = - R_{\\alpha i}^2/U_{\\alpha i}^2$, linking light neutrino masses $m_i$, heavy neutrino masses $M_i$, elements of the heavy-light mixing matrix $R_{\\alpha i}$, and PMNS matrix elements $U_{\\alpha i}$. They then derive implications for CP-violating phases.
\n\n
作者在“按代分离”假设下求解精确seesaw关系 $m_i = - (M_i R_{\\alpha i}^2)/U_{\\alpha i}^2$，将每一对轻-重中微子 $(i)$ 独立处理，得到条件 $m_i/M_i = - R_{\\alpha i}^2/U_{\\alpha i}^2$，将轻中微子质量 $m_i$、重中微子质量 $M_i$、轻重混合矩阵元 $R_{\\alpha i}$ 与PMNS矩阵元 $U_{\\alpha i}$ 联系起来，并由此推导出对CP破坏相位的含义。

Result: The family-separated ansatz leads to explicit algebraic relations among seesaw parameters, enabling a direct mapping between low-energy CP violation in neutrino oscillations (governed by the Dirac phase $\\delta$ in the PMNS matrix) and high-scale CP asymmetries in heavy Majorana neutrino decays—offering a falsifiable prediction within low-scale seesaw frameworks.
\n\n
“按代分离”假设导出了seesaw参数间的显式代数关系，使得低能中微子振荡中的CP破坏（由PMNS矩阵中的Dirac相位 $\\delta$ 主导）与高能标重中微子衰变中的CP不对称性之间存在直接映射——为低能标seesaw框架提供了可证伪的预言。

Conclusion: This work demonstrates that non-generic but theoretically consistent solutions to the seesaw equation can significantly sharpen the phenomenological predictions of neutrino mass models, particularly by establishing a concrete bridge between observable CP violation in experiments and underlying high-scale lepton-number-violating dynamics.
\n\n
本工作表明，seesaw方程的非普适但理论自洽的解能够显著增强中微子质量模型的现象学预言能力，特别是通过在实验可观测的CP破坏与底层高能标轻子数破坏动力学之间建立具体桥梁。

Abstract: Given the canonical seesaw mechanism as a most natural extension of the standard model in its neutrino sector, we find out a special but brand new solution to the exact seesaw equation: $m^{}_i/M^{}_i = - R^2_{αi}/U^2_{αi}$ for the masses and flavor mixing matrix elements of light and heavy Majorana neutrinos of the $i$-th family (for $i = 1, 2, 3$ and $α= e, μ, τ$). This family-separated seesaw scenario allows us to establish simple relations between the original seesaw parameters and the active degrees of freedom, and thus offers some testable predictions such as a direct correlation between the CP-violating effects in light neutrino flavor oscillations and heavy Majorana neutrino decays.

</details>

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper performs a joint analysis of CE$\\nu$NS data from COHERENT (using stopped-pion neutrinos) and CONUS+ (using reactor antineutrinos) on germanium targets to simultaneously constrain the neutron skin, neutron root-mean-square radius, and the weak mixing angle at low momentum transfer. By combining complementary datasets, the authors reduce systematic uncertainties—particularly those related to normalization and spectral shape—and present results as functions of the Lindhard quenching model. Nuclear structure effects are evaluated using the Klein-Nystrand form factor benchmarked against Shell Model calculations.

本文对COHERENT（使用静止π介子衰变产生的中微子）和CONUS+（使用反应堆反中微子）在锗靶上的CE$\\nu$NS数据进行了联合分析，同时约束中子皮厚度、中子均方根半径以及低动量转移下的弱混合角。通过结合互补数据集，作者降低了与归一化和谱形相关的系统误差，并将结果表示为Lindhard淬灭模型的函数。核结构效应通过Klein-Nystrand形状因子进行评估，并与Shell Model计算结果进行基准比对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to improve constraints on nuclear structure parameters (neutron skin and rms neutron radius) and test the Standard Model via precision measurement of the weak mixing angle at low $Q^2$, a regime poorly probed by previous experiments. The complementarity between stopped-pion and reactor neutrino spectra helps break degeneracies between normalization uncertainties and spectral distortions inherent in CE$\\nu$NS measurements.
\n\n
动机在于改进对核结构参数（中子皮和中子均方根半径）的约束，并通过在低$Q^2$区域精确测量弱混合角来检验Standard Model——该区域此前实验探测较少。静止π介子中微子与反应堆中微子谱的互补性有助于打破CE$\\nu$NS测量中归一化不确定性和谱形畸变之间的简并。

Method: The authors conduct a joint likelihood analysis of COHERENT and CONUS+ CE$\\nu$NS data on germanium. They model the differential cross section using the Standard Model prediction with a vector weak charge $Q_W = N - (1 - 4 \\sin^2 \\theta_W) Z$, incorporate the Klein-Nystrand nuclear form factor, and account for detector response via the Lindhard quenching factor. Systematic uncertainties from quenching and nuclear modeling are explored by varying the Lindhard model parameters and comparing the Klein-Nystrand form factor to large-scale Shell Model predictions. The fit simultaneously extracts $\\langle r_n^2 \\rangle^{1/2}$, neutron skin $\\Delta R_{np}$, and $\\sin^2 \\theta_W$.
\n\n
作者对COHERENT和CONUS+在锗上的CE$\\nu$NS数据进行联合似然分析。他们使用Standard Model预测的微分截面，其中矢量弱电荷为$Q_W = N - (1 - 4 \\sin^2 \\theta_W) Z$，采用Klein-Nystrand核形状因子，并通过Lindhard淬灭因子描述探测器响应。通过改变Lindhard模型参数并将Klein-Nystrand形状因子与大规模Shell Model预测进行比较，探索淬灭效应和核模型带来的系统误差。拟合同时提取$\\langle r_n^2 \\rangle^{1/2}$、中子皮$\\Delta R_{np}$和$\\sin^2 \\theta_W$。

Result: The joint analysis yields improved precision on the germanium neutron skin $\\Delta R_{np}$ and rms neutron radius compared to individual experiments, and provides a competitive determination of $\\sin^2 \\theta_W$ at low momentum transfer ($Q^2 \\sim \\mathcal{O}(10^{-2})~\\text{GeV}^2$). Results are presented as functions of the Lindhard quenching parameter $k$, showing sensitivity to nuclear recoil modeling. The Klein-Nystrand form factor is validated against Shell Model calculations, confirming its adequacy for current experimental precision while quantifying residual nuclear structure uncertainties.
\n\n
联合分析相比单个实验显著提高了锗中子皮$\\Delta R_{np}$和中子均方根半径的精度，并在低动量转移（$Q^2 \\sim \\mathcal{O}(10^{-2})~\\text{GeV}^2$）下给出了具有竞争力的$\\sin^2 \\theta_W$测量结果。结果以Lindhard淬灭参数$k$为函数呈现，显示出对核反冲模型的敏感性。Klein-Nystrand形状因子经与Shell Model计算对比验证，确认其在当前实验精度下适用，并量化了残余的核结构不确定性。

Conclusion: Combining CE$\\nu$NS data from distinct neutrino sources (stopped-pion and reactor) significantly enhances sensitivity to both nuclear structure and electroweak physics by breaking key degeneracies. This approach demonstrates a path toward precision CE$\\nu$NS phenomenology, where control over quenching models and nuclear form factors is essential. Future measurements with reduced systematics will further probe BSM physics and neutron distributions in nuclei.
\n\n
结合来自不同中微子源（静止π介子和反应堆）的CE$\\nu$NS数据，通过打破关键简并，显著提升了对核结构和电弱物理的灵敏度。该方法展示了通往高精度CE$\\nu$NS现象学的路径，其中对淬灭模型和核形状因子的控制至关重要。未来系统误差更低的测量将进一步探查BSM物理和原子核中的中子分布。

Abstract: We present a combined analysis of recent CE$ν$NS data on germanium from two complementary experiments: COHERENT, which uses neutrinos from pion decay at rest, and CONUS+, which detects reactor antineutrinos. Exploiting the complementarity of these two datasets in a joint statistical analysis, we extract the germanium root-mean-square neutron radius and neutron skin with improved precision, disentangling spectral shape distortions from overall normalizations and reducing systematic uncertainties. We also determine the weak mixing angle at low momentum transfer, providing a test of the Standard Model in a less-explored kinematic regime. A key systematic uncertainty in CE$ν$NS ionization measurements is the nuclear quenching factor; we therefore present our results as a function of variations of the Lindhard model. For the nuclear form factor, we adopt the analytical Klein-Nystrand parametrization and benchmark it against predictions from the large-scale nuclear Shell Model, assessing the impact of nuclear structure uncertainties on our results. Our analysis demonstrates the power of combining datasets across different neutrino sources to maximize sensitivity to both nuclear and electroweak physics.

### [72] [Polarization dependence of the $φ$ meson mass from finite-temperature QCD sum rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27347)
*Hidefumi Matsuda,Philipp Gubler,Koichi Hattori*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: The paper investigates in-medium properties of the $\\phi$ meson at finite temperature and momentum via QCD sum rules, revealing a momentum- and temperature-dependent transverse–longitudinal mass splitting driven primarily by dimension-four spin-dependent thermal condensates.
\n\n
本文利用QCD求和规则研究了有限温度和有限动量下$\\phi$介子的介质内性质，揭示了由四维自旋依赖热凝聚主导的、随动量和温度变化的横向-纵向质量分裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: At finite temperature and density, hadronic properties are modified due to interactions with the surrounding medium. Understanding these modifications—especially for vector mesons like the $\\phi$ meson—is crucial for interpreting heavy-ion collision experiments and probing chiral symmetry restoration. The breaking of Lorentz invariance in medium necessitates separate treatment of transverse and longitudinal polarization modes, motivating a detailed QCD sum rule analysis incorporating thermal condensates.
\n\n
在有限温度和密度下，强子性质会因与周围介质的相互作用而发生改变。理解这些修正——特别是对于$\\phi$矢量介子——对解释重离子碰撞实验和探索手征对称性恢复至关重要。介质中洛伦兹不变性的破缺要求对横向和纵向极化模式分别处理，这促使作者通过包含热凝聚项的QCD求和规则进行详细分析。

Method: The authors employ finite-temperature QCD sum rules (QCD SR) to compute the in-medium correlation function of the $\\phi$ meson current. They decompose the correlator into transverse and longitudinal components relative to the meson's three-momentum. Operator Product Expansion (OPE) is used up to dimension-four operators, including temperature-dependent quark and gluon condensates. Special attention is given to spin-dependent dimension-four thermal condensates (e.g., $\\langle \\bar{q} \\sigma_{\\mu\\nu} q \\rangle_T$ and $\\langle G_{\\mu\\alpha} G_\\nu^{\\ \\alpha} \\rangle_T$), which break rotational symmetry and generate mode splitting. Borel transformation is applied to suppress higher-energy contributions and enhance pole terms.
\n\n
作者采用有限温度下的QCD求和规则（QCD SR）计算$\\phi$介子流的介质内关联函数。他们将关联函数按介子三维动量方向分解为横向和纵向分量。使用算符乘积展开（OPE），包含至四维算符，并计入温度依赖的夸克和胶子凝聚。特别关注四维自旋依赖热凝聚（如$\\langle \\bar{q} \\sigma_{\\mu\\nu} q \\rangle_T$和$\\langle G_{\\mu\\alpha} G_\\nu^{\\ \\alpha} \\rangle_T$），这些项破坏旋转对称性并导致模式分裂。应用Borel变换以压低高能连续谱贡献并增强极点项。

Result: The analysis shows that both transverse and longitudinal effective masses of the $\\phi$ meson increase with momentum. A distinct transverse–longitudinal mass splitting emerges, which grows with increasing temperature. This splitting is dominantly attributed to dimension-four spin-dependent thermal condensates, while lower-dimensional condensates contribute equally to both modes and thus do not induce splitting.
\n\n
分析表明，$\\phi$介子的横向和纵向有效质量均随动量增加而增大。明显的横向-纵向质量分裂随之出现，且该分裂随温度升高而增大。这一分裂主要归因于四维自旋依赖热凝聚；而低维凝聚对两种模式的贡献相同，因此不产生分裂。

Conclusion: The study demonstrates that Lorentz symmetry breaking in hot QCD matter leads to observable polarization-dependent modifications of vector meson properties. The transverse–longitudinal mass splitting of the $\\phi$ meson serves as a sensitive probe of spin-dependent thermal condensates, offering insights into non-perturbative QCD dynamics at finite temperature and implications for dilepton production in heavy-ion collisions.
\n\n
研究表明，高温QCD物质中的洛伦兹对称性破缺会导致矢量介子性质出现可观测的偏振依赖修正。$\\phi$介子的横向-纵向质量分裂可作为探测自旋依赖热凝聚的灵敏探针，为有限温度下的非微扰QCD动力学提供洞见，并对重离子碰撞中的双轻子产额具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study the $φ$ meson at finite temperature and finite momentum using QCD sum rules. The presence of medium breaks the Lorentz invariance, and induces distinct in-medium modifications of the transverse and longitudinal modes at finite momentum. We find that, with increasing momentum, the masses of both modes increase and a clear transverse--longitudinal splitting develops. The splitting is found to grow with temperature and to be mainly generated by the dimension-four spin-dependent thermal condensates.
